Abstract: Tool-augmented multimodal reasoning integrates external tools (e.g., object detection, depth estimation) into multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to address perceptual bottlenecks in complex visual tasks. However, existing approaches rarely verify tool outputs, limiting their ability to detect and recover from tool failures. We propose ReVISE, a framework that equips MLLMs with verification and dynamic error recovery for tool-augmented reasoning. ReVISE introduces (1) a curated training dataset that supervises reflective behaviors, enabling models to validate tool-derived evidence, reformulate queries when visual mismatches arise, and fall back to intrinsic grounding when external tools are unreliable; and (2) a reinforcement learning based targeted rewards that encourage internal reflection and penalize spatial misalignment. Experiments on several benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over existing methods, highlighting the importance of error detection and correction in tool-augmented multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [105] [FiLM-GPNet: Geometry-Aware Pseudo-Supervised Phase Restoration with Zero-Shot Generalization for Large Temporal InSAR Stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29384)
*Getnet Demil,Muhammad Farhan Humayun,Tomi Westerlund,Jukka Heikkonen,Mourad Oussalah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FiLM-GPNet，一种基于几何条件（FiLM调制+7D几何描述符）的SAR干涉相位恢复网络，通过Goldstein滤波伪监督和干涉物理三闭环一致性正则化训练，并估计逐像素aleatoric不确定性。在Capella Spotlight三个数据集上，相比Goldstein基线时间残差降低68%/66%，闭合成环误差降低10%/13%，并展示出对地理和几何上不同区域的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集商业SAR时间序列的普及使得时间维InSAR分析成为可能，但固定经典滤波器（如Goldstein滤波）在异构采集几何条件下失效，导致相位质量下降和时间一致性受损。需要一个能显式适应采集几何差异的相位恢复方法。

Method: 1) 使用特征线性调制（FiLM）让网络根据7D per-pair几何描述符自适应调整特征映射；2) 用Goldstein滤波干涉图作为伪标签进行监督训练；3) 通过三闭环（triplet-closure）一致性约束正则化模型，嵌入干涉物理原理；4) 同时估计逐像素aleatoric不确定性；5) 在Capella Spotlight三个堆栈（夏威夷、西澳、洛杉矶）上评估，其中洛杉矶仅用于零样本泛化测试。

Result: 夏威夷和西澳相比Goldstein基线时间残差分别降低68%和66%；闭合成环误差分别降低10%和13%；西澳解缠成功率提升7.7个百分点，DEM NMAD改善31%；对洛杉矶第三堆栈零样本泛化表现良好。

Conclusion: 几何条件化的相位恢复是固定经典滤波在异构堆栈上的有效替代方案，FiLM机制能显式适应采集几何差异，且模型具备跨区域零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: The growing availability of dense commercial Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) time series enables temporal Interferometric SAR (InSAR) analysis, but fixed classical filters fail under heterogeneous acquisition geometries, degrading phase quality and temporal consistency. We propose FiLM-GPNet, a geometry-conditioned network for wrapped-phase restoration that explicitly adapts to acquisition differences using Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) and a 7D per-pair geometry descriptor. The model is trained with pseudo-supervision from Goldstein-filtered interferograms and regularized by interferometric physics via triplet-closure consistency, while also estimating per-pixel aleatoric uncertainty. Experiments on three Capella Spotlight stacks from the IEEE GRSS 2026 Data Fusion Contest show that FiLM-GPNet reduces temporal residual by 68% (Hawaii) and 66% (Western Australia) relative to the Goldstein baseline, alongside closure error reductions of 10% and 13%, respectively. In Western Australia, it further improves unwrapping success rate by 7.7 percentage points and Digital Elevation Model (DEM) Normalized Median Absolute Deviation (NMAD) by 31%. The model also shows strong zero-shot generalization to a geographically and geometrically distinct third stack (Los Angeles) without retraining, supporting geometry-conditioned restoration as an effective alternative to fixed classical filtering across heterogeneous stacks.

</details>


### [106] [GATE: Reliability-Gated Gaussian Evidence Fusion for Training-Free Test-Time Adaptation of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29395)
*Pedram MohajerAnsari,Amir Salarpour,Run Wang,Mert D. Pesé*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GATE 是一种无需训练的、两遍式转导测试时自适应框架，通过构建文本高斯和图像高斯两种互补证据源，并引入类级可靠性门控和广义 Product-of-Experts 融合，在完全冻结视觉编码器、文本编码器和提示参数的条件下，提升 CLIP/SigLIP 等视觉-语言模型在分布偏移下的零样本识别鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP/SigLIP 等视觉-语言模型在预训练分布上表现优异，但在目标数据分布偏移下预测性能会下降。现有测试时自适应方法要么仅依赖提示侧（prompt-side）适应，要么仅依赖图像侧目标证据，缺乏对两种证据的互补利用。作者希望在不访问源数据、不使用目标标签、且完全冻结模型参数的前提下，提升测试时鲁棒性。

Method: GATE 采用两遍式转导测试时自适应流程：(1) 第一遍用零样本预测筛选可靠的无标签目标样本；(2) 在共享视觉-语言特征空间中构建两类互补证据——文本高斯（由多个语言描述估计）和图像高斯（由可靠目标样本估计）；(3) 类级可靠性门控控制图像伪证据的影响权重；(4) 通过得分级别的广义 Product-of-Experts（gPoE）融合生成对原始零样本 logits 的归一化残差修正。整个过程无需训练，模型参数全部冻结。

Result: 在细粒度识别数据集、ImageNet 系列分布偏移、多个 CLIP 骨干网络及 SigLIP-B/16 上，GATE 在每个基准/骨干组合中均取得最佳平均准确率。相比零样本基线平均提升 5.41 个百分点，相比最强非 GATE 基线提升 1.94 个百分点，验证了可靠性门控的分布性证据对冻结 VLM 自适应的有效性。

Conclusion: GATE 证明在完全冻结的视觉-语言模型中，通过融合文本端与图像端的分布性证据（高斯建模）并辅以可靠性门控，可以在不更新任何参数的情况下显著超越现有测试时自适应方法，为高效友好的 VLM 部署提供了一种训练无关的实用方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models such as CLIP and SigLIP provide strong zero-shot recognition, but their predictions can degrade when deployed on target data that differ from the pretraining distribution. Test-time adaptation offers a practical way to improve robustness without source data or target labels, yet existing methods often rely on either prompt-side adaptation or image-side target evidence alone. In this work, we introduce GATE, a training-free two-pass transductive test-time adaptation framework that uses the unlabeled target set while keeping the image encoder, text encoder, and prompt parameters fully frozen. Instead of representing each class with a single prototype, GATE builds two complementary Gaussian sources of evidence in the shared vision-language feature space: a text Gaussian estimated from multiple language descriptions and an image Gaussian estimated from reliable unlabeled target samples. A class-wise reliability gate controls the influence of image-derived pseudo-evidence, and a score-level generalized Product-of-Experts fusion produces a normalized residual correction to the original zero-shot logits. Across fine-grained recognition datasets, ImageNet-family distribution shifts, multiple CLIP backbones, and SigLIP-B/16, GATE achieves the best average accuracy in every benchmark/backbone group. It improves zero-shot performance by an average of 5.41 points and outperforms the strongest non-GATE baseline by 1.94 points, demonstrating the benefit of reliability-gated distributional evidence for frozen VLM adaptation.

</details>


### [107] [A Visual Question Answering Model to Automate Nondestructive Evaluation Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29408)
*Mehrdad Shafiei Dizaji,Hoda Azari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向无损检测（NDE）应用的视觉问答（VQA）模型，结合ResNet-50提取图像特征和GPT-2生成语言，使检测人员能够以自然语言交互方式查询NDE图像（如"是否存在裂纹"、"缺陷位置在哪里"），从而提升检测效率、减少误差并增强现场实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的无损检测通常依赖人工判读或固定规则的自动化方法，交互性差、效率低且容易出错。本文引入VQA技术，使检测人员能够以自然语言问答方式直观地查询NDE图像，提升检测流程的交互性和实用性。

Method: 采用编码器-解码器架构：图像编码部分使用ResNet-50进行视觉特征提取，语言生成部分使用GPT-2，将图像特征与问题文本融合后生成精确答案，实现对NDE图像的问答式交互。

Result: 该模型能够让检测人员对NDE图像进行自然语言交互式查询（如缺陷是否存在、缺陷位置），并返回准确回答，提升了检测效率并减少了人为误差。

Conclusion: 该VQA模型为NDE应用提供了一种新的交互式检测范式，通过问答方式降低检测门槛、提高效率，具有实际工程应用价值。

Abstract: This study introduces a Visual Question Answering model designed specifically for nondestructive evaluation applications. VQA models allow inspectors to interactively query NDE images, asking targeted questions like, Is there a crack or Where is the defect located and receive precise answers from the model. Leveraging deep learning and natural language processing, the developed system integrates image feature extraction (via a ResNet-50 model) and language generation capabilities (via GPT-2) to provide accurate, informative feedback. By enabling direct question-and-answer interactions, this VQA model significantly improves inspection efficiency, reduces potential errors, and enhances usability in practical field scenarios.

</details>


### [108] [Polis: 3D Self-Supervision at City Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29426)
*Alexander Rusnak,Sophia Kovalenko,Jingru Wang,Ismail Moudden,Xiru Wang,Frédéric Kaplan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Polis，首次将Sketched Isotropic Gaussian Regularization (SIGReg)应用于原生点云编码器的自监督目标，结合几何匹配余弦不变量、VICReg风格防塌缩项，在12.8k场景的户外预训练混合数据和重力保持空间视图采样上训练。在14个城市/建筑尺度数据集上的冻结特征基准测试中，Polis在三个预训练不相交的城市数据集上达到23.8%平均mIoU（次优编码器为16.3%），但在精细地面激光扫描数据上排名发生反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模城市3D模型的可靠语义表示对城市分析、基础设施监控、自动驾驶和遗产保护越来越重要，但城市级航测点云与现有3D自监督模型预训练所用的室内、物体级、自动驾驶LiDAR数据差异巨大，现有方法难以直接迁移。

Method: Polis采用SIGReg作为原生点云编码器的训练目标，结合几何匹配的余弦不变量、VICReg风格防塌缩项，使用12.8k场景户外预训练混合数据和重力保持的空间视图采样进行自监督预训练，然后通过冻结特征探测评估下游性能。

Result: 在三个预训练未见过的城市数据集上，Polis达到23.8%平均mIoU vs 次优模型的16.3%（高容量冻结探针）、17.3% vs 16.1%（匹配点体和体素预算）。在预训练见过的城市数据上优势保持，但在精细地面激光扫描细粒度标注数据上排名反转。

Conclusion: 分布正则化的联合嵌入架构能在挑战性的城市场景中成功，当自监督设计匹配捕获几何和空间上下文时迁移性能提升，但也揭示了这种领域特化的局限性（异地构建数据上表现不佳）。

Abstract: Reliable semantic representations derived from city-scale 3D models are increasingly important for urban analysis, infrastructure monitoring, autonomous systems, and heritage conservation. However, urban scenes of large spatial extent captured through aerial surveying differ substantially from the indoor, object-level, and self-driving LiDAR data used to pretrain most 3D self-supervised models. We introduce Polis, to our knowledge the first application of Sketched Isotropic Gaussian Regularization (SIGReg) as an objective for a native point cloud encoder, and evaluate it through a frozen-feature benchmark spanning fourteen city- and building-scale corpora. Polis combines geometrically matched cosine invariance, SIGReg, and VICReg-style anti-collapse terms with a 12.8k-scene outdoor pretraining mixture and gravity-preserving spatial view sampling. Controlled ablations show that this objective outperforms student--teacher architecture alternatives, as well as Polis versions without anti-collapse terms, on the same representative outdoor corpus. On three pretraining-disjoint city datasets, Polis reaches $23.8\%$ mean mIoU versus $16.3\%$ for the next-best encoder under high-capacity frozen probing, and $17.3\%$ versus $16.1\%$ at a matched point and voxel budget. The same city-scale lead holds on datasets whose training sets were seen in pretraining. On localized terrestrial captures with fine-grained facade and streetscape labels, the ranking reverses. Our results show that distributionally-regularized joint embedding architectures can be successful on challenging city-scale 3D scenes, and that transfer improves when self-supervision is designed for the capture geometry and spatial context of this domain while also revealing the limits of this specialization.

</details>


### [109] [Text-Guided Diffusion-Based Adversarial Attacks on Chest X-Ray Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29456)
*Basudha Pal,Arjun Narayanan,Neha Ajith,Vikas R Bhat,Muhammad Umair*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于文本引导扩散模型的对抗攻击框架，通过优化可学习的文本条件而非直接像素扰动来生成对抗性胸部X光图像，在多疾病分类任务中显著降低模型性能，同时保持图像真实性和临床医生判读不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学影像AI鲁棒性评估主要依赖像素空间攻击（如FGSM、PGD、C&W），这类攻击虽然有效但产生的人为噪声不代表真实的影像学变异，且在多疾病CXR分类场景难以揭示更符合临床语义的对抗失败模式。

Method: 提出文本引导的扩散对抗框架：冻结扩散生成器和目标分类器，仅优化可学习的文本条件向量，利用学习到的图像先验生成对抗样本，而非直接操作像素。在二元肺不张和多元疾病CXR分类上评估多个分类器架构，与FGSM、PGD、C&W对比。

Result: 该方法在二元分类中将AUROC降至0.3885-0.5646，多疾病设置降至0.4441-0.4878，均优于基准攻击；同时保持高图像保真度（SSIM 0.9080, LPIPS 0.1670, FID 51.23）。95.9%二元和73.8%多疾病的对抗图像临床判读未变，但模型预测显著改变。

Conclusion: 揭示人类与机器对医学影像解读之间存在关键差异，主张医学AI鲁棒性评估需从像素空间攻击扩展至生成式威胁模型，以暴露在视觉和临床维度上合理的变化下的模型失效。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence is increasingly integrated into chest X-ray (CXR) interpretation, triage, and clinical decision support, understanding its vulnerability to adversarial manipulation is critical for safe deployment. Existing robustness evaluations, however, predominantly rely on pixel-space attacks that introduce numerically constrained perturbations but may not represent plausible radiographic variation. This limitation is particularly important in multi-disease CXR classification, where models simultaneously evaluate multiple overlapping pathologies and adversarial failures may alter several diagnostic predictions. We propose a text-guided diffusion-based adversarial framework that optimizes learnable text conditioning while keeping the diffusion generator and target classifier frozen, enabling adversarial generation through a learned image prior rather than direct pixel manipulation. We evaluate the framework across multiple classifier architectures in both binary atelectasis and multi-disease CXR classification and compare it with FGSM, PGD, and Carlini-Wagner attacks. Our approach consistently produced the greatest degradation in classifier performance, reducing AUROC to 0.3885-0.5646 in binary classification and 0.4441-0.4878 in the multi-disease setting, while achieving superior image fidelity (SSIM 0.9080, LPIPS 0.1670, FID 51.23). Importantly, clinician interpretation remained unchanged for 95.9% of binary and 73.8% of multi-disease adversarial images despite substantial changes in model predictions. These findings reveal a clinically important discrepancy between human and machine interpretation and demonstrate the need to extend medical AI robustness evaluation beyond conventional pixel-space attacks toward generative threat models that can expose failures under visually and clinically plausible image variations.

</details>


### [110] [Co-Evolutionary Prompt Optimization with Cross-Category Transfer for Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29467)
*Sisi Zhu,Changwei Yu,Renshuai Tao,Zhenliang Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoEvoAD提出了一种基于共进化框架的零样本异常检测方法，使用进化算法在离散自然语言空间中搜索提示词，并通过跨类别迁移目标（CCTO）提升泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CLIP-based零样本异常检测方法通常使用连续提示嵌入进行提示优化，将语义编码在潜在向量中，缺乏可解释性和可扩展性。论文旨在通过离散自然语言空间的提示搜索来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出CoEvoAD共进化框架：1）使用进化算法在离散自然语言空间中迭代生成、评估和选择候选提示词，保持自然语言的可解释性和可组合性；2）引入跨类别迁移目标（CCTO），将held-out源类别作为未见类别的代理，根据估计的跨类别可迁移性对提示规则打分，提升跨类别泛化能力。

Result: 在多个异常检测数据集上取得state-of-the-art性能，验证了CoEvoAD的有效性，代码已开源。

Conclusion: CoEvoAD通过离散提示进化搜索和跨类别迁移目标，实现了可解释且可扩展的零样本异常检测，显著提升了跨类别泛化性能。

Abstract: Zero-shot anomaly detection (ZSAD) has gained significant attention for its practical value in industrial inspection. Recently, CLIP-based approaches have been widely adopted in ZSAD due to their strong vision-language generalization capabilities. However, existing methods commonly employ continuous prompt embeddings for prompt optimization and encode semantics in latent vectors, which lack interpretability and scalability. To this end, we propose CoEvoAD, a co-evolutionary framework for discrete prompt selection. CoEvoAD performs prompt search in the discrete natural-language space using an evolutionary algorithm. Candidate prompts are iteratively generated, evaluated, and selected throughout population evolution, thus preserving the interpretability and composability of natural language. Furthermore, we introduce a Cross-Category Transfer Objective (CCTO), which treats held-out source categories as proxies for unseen categories and scores prompt rules based on their estimated cross-category transferability, effectively improving cross-category generalization. Extensive experiments are conducted to validate the effectiveness of CoEvoAD, and the results show that it achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple anomaly detection datasets. The code is available at https://github.com/rstao-bjtu/CoEvoAD.

</details>


### [111] [Seeing Through Extreme Visual Sparsity: Surface Understanding from a Single Random Visual Patch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29475)
*Sindhuja Penchala,Sudip Mittal,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SSUF（稀疏表面理解框架），一个统一的双任务学习框架，将四种预训练架构（ConvAE、ViT、Swin Transformer、MAE）适配为同时进行表面重建和材料分类。在Touch-and-Go数据集上使用仅10%可见像素的稀疏观测协议进行实验。结果表明Swin Transformer分类最优（准确率89.21%），MAE重建最优（PSNR 16.06 dB），ViT在重建与分类之间取得最佳平衡，所有模型推理时间均小于5ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人感知和环境理解中，从不完整的视觉观测中识别表面材料仍然是一个具有挑战性的问题。现有方法通常只能处理完整的视觉输入，而真实世界中的机器人常面临遮挡或稀疏观测的场景。需要一个统一的框架来评估不同预训练架构在稀疏条件下同时完成表面重建与材料分类的能力。

Method: SSUF框架采用双任务学习策略：将四种预训练架构统一适配，重建导向模型（如MAE、ConvAE）扩展分类头，分类导向模型（如ViT、Swin）增加重建解码器。在Touch-and-Go数据集上采用稀疏观测协议（仅保留10%可见像素，其余masked），从重建质量、分类性能、模型复杂度和推理效率四个维度全面评估各架构。

Result: Swin Transformer在分类上表现最佳：准确率89.21%、F1-score 0.8922、ROC-AUC 0.9813。MAE取得最高重建分数：PSNR 16.06 dB、SSIM 0.4501。ViT在重建与分类间取得最佳平衡。所有模型均实现实时推理（<5ms/图）。

Conclusion: 预训练架构能够在严重视觉稀疏条件下支持材料识别，但精确的图像重建仍然具有挑战性。不同架构在双任务上展现出互补优势，SSUF框架为稀疏表面理解提供了统一的比较基线和研究范式。

Abstract: Surface material recognition from incomplete visual observations remains a challenging problem in robotic perception and environmental understanding. This paper discusses Sparse Surface Understanding Framework (SSUF), a unified dual-task learning framework that adapts four pretrained architectures-Convolutional Autoencoder (ConvAE), Vision Transformer (ViT), Swin Transformer, and Masked Autoencoder (MAE) for si-multaneous surface reconstruction and material classification. Experiments were conducted on the Touch-and-Go dataset using a sparse observation protocol in which only 10% of the original image remained visible while the remaining regions were masked. To enable a fair comparison, reconstruction-oriented models were extended with classification heads, whereas classification- oriented models were augmented with reconstruction decoders. The resulting architectures were assessed using reconstruction quality, classification performance, model complexity, and in-ference efficiency metrics. Experimental results revealed distinct strengths across the models. Swin Transformer achieved the best classification performance with an accuracy of 89.21%, an F1-score of 0.8922, and a ROC-AUC of 0.9813. In contrast, MAE produced the highest reconstruction scores among evaluated models, with a PSNR of 16.06 dB and an SSIM of 0.4501, while ViT provided the best overall balance between reconstruction and classification performance. Furthermore, all models achieved real-time inference, requiring less than 5 ms per image. Over-all, the results show that pretrained architectures can support material recognition under severe visual sparsity, while accurate image reconstruction remains challenging.

</details>


### [112] [GeoAgent: Evaluating VLM Geolocalization Through Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29483)
*Arka Mukherjee,Soham Roy,Kartikeya Trivedi,Shreya Ghosh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoAgent，一个基于具身导航的地理定位基准，要求多模态智能体在Street View环境中自主探索并逐步推理来完成地理定位，而非仅依赖静态图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在地理定位任务上的研究局限于静态图像检索、分类和预测，未能忠实还原该任务——即通过具身导航在现实环境中收集观测后再做出预测。作者认为真实的地理定位应包含具身导航过程。

Method: 构建GeoAgent基准环境，让多模态智能体在Street View场景中自主导航探索，通过序列化推理逐步收敛地理定位预测。包含环境、评测协议以及与静态图像基线的对比分析。

Result: 发现现代VLM在国家和洲级预测表现好，但难以辨别区域级模式；具身导航相比静态图像基线在多项指标上显著提升准确率；同时发现前沿模型存在发达国家/发展中国家区域的严重偏见，以及在错误先验下自我改进能力差。

Conclusion: 具身导航和地理空间推理仍是挑战性问题，GeoAgent为VLM地理定位能力提供了更真实的评测视角，并公开了代码与环境。

Abstract: Modern Vision-Language Models (VLMs) perform well above the human baseline in image geolocalization, a task critically important in disaster response, OSINT verification, and location privacy. However, most efforts to study AI behavior on the task remain limited to static image-based retrieval, classification, and predictions. We argue that faithful recreation of the task should involve embodied navigation, where a multimodal agent autonomously explores its surroundings to gather observations before submitting a prediction. To this end, we introduce \textbf{GeoAgent}, an agentic environment-based benchmark that requires agents to navigate Street View environments to refine their geolocalization through sequential reasoning. Our analysis shows that modern VLMs struggle to discern regional patterns while succeeding at country- and continent-level predictions. When compared to static image-based baselines, agentic navigation significantly improves accuracy across established metrics. We also note severe bias in a developed/developing region context across frontier model architectures and poor self-improvement capabilities given incorrect priors. Overall, our work establishes the challenges of embodied navigation and geospatial reasoning. We publicly release our code and the GeoAgent environment: https://geoagent-benchmark.github.io

</details>


### [113] [ARMOR: Manifold-Oriented Training for Adversarially Robust Aerial Object Detection under Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29510)
*Haoran Wang,Matthew Lau,Alec Helbling,Matthew Hull,ShengYun Peng,Mansi Phute,Martin Andreoni,Willian T. Lunardi,Duen Horng Chau,Wenke Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ARMOR是一种面向低数据场景的敌对鲁棒性防御方法，通过掩码图像背景保留目标相关特征，并在目标上注入随机补丁来提升特征鲁棒性，从而在不依赖大规模生成模型和数据增强训练的条件下，实现对抗补丁攻击下的鲁棒目标检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中目标检测模型易受物理对抗补丁攻击导致漏检，而防御方法通常假设拥有数万张训练图像，但实际部署场景中航拍数据采集和标注昂贵，通常只有数百张图像。作者希望同时解决模型脆弱性和训练数据稀缺的双重挑战。

Method: ARMOR基于流形取向对抗训练（OMAT）的核心思想，但采用数据高效策略：(i) 掩码图像背景以保留对象相关特征（利用检测任务已有的标签），(ii) 在对象上注入随机补丁以提升特征鲁棒性。在低数据条件下同时评估查询无关迁移攻击和防御感知攻击。

Result: ARMOR在保持超过0.90模型置信度的强干净性能的同时，相比最先进防御在模型置信度上提升最多0.32的对抗鲁棒性。物理实验用打印补丁证实了这些增益在真实部署中依然有效。

Conclusion: ARMOR成功将流形训练的思想转化为数据匮乏条件下的可实践防御方案，为低数据航拍目标检测提供了兼顾干净性能与对抗鲁棒性的解决方案。

Abstract: Aerial object detection is increasingly deployed in real-world applications, but models remain vulnerable to physical, universal adversarial patches that cause them to miss objects. Furthermore, defenders face the practical constraint of training data scarcity: aerial imagery is costly to collect and label, so a deployment site typically yields hundreds of images rather than the tens of thousands that adversarial robustness benchmarks assume. To tackle model vulnerability and training data scarcity, we propose Adversarial Robustness with Manifold-Oriented Training (ARMOR), a novel defense that realizes the core insights of on-manifold adversarial training (OMAT) in low-data regimes. ARMOR builds on the insight of OMAT to model the data manifold - the compact structure capturing the data's relevant features - to learn and robustify these features during training. While OMAT relies on the data-intensive operations of training large generative models and adversarial training to achieve this, ARMOR adopts a data-efficient approach that reuses labels the detection task already supplies: ARMOR (i) masks image backgrounds to retain object-relevant features, and (ii) injects randomized patches on objects to improve feature robustness. Our low-data experiments with physically-realizable adversarial patches evaluate both query-free transfer attacks and defense-aware attacks. ARMOR maintains strong clean performance of over 0.90 model confidence, while improving adversarial robustness by up to 0.32 in model confidence over state-of-the-art defenses. Physical experiments with printed patches confirm that these gains survive deployment. Overall, ARMOR translates insights from manifold-based training to defend object detectors amidst training data scarcity.

</details>


### [114] [FuncRoom-Agent: Sequential Feed-Forward 3D Functional Indoor Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29519)
*Hao Feng,Zhi Zuo,MingJian Liang,Jingyu Hu,Xiaowei Hu,Liupengfei Wu,Dian Zhang,Guoxin Fang,Zhengzhe Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种新的室内三维场景生成设置——功能房间生成（Function-Room Generation），通过递归DSL语言、顺序前馈场景构建框架和基于执行的过程奖励框架ScenePRM，在无需生成-评估-修正循环的情况下高效生成满足明确功能目标的室内场景，并在通用与功能房间生成基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可执行/智能体式室内场景生成方法虽然提升了可控性，但依赖昂贵的测试时"生成-评估-修正"循环，导致功能房间生成速度慢、计算开销大。需要一种无需迭代修正的高效功能场景生成方法。

Method: 1) 设计递归领域特定语言（DSL），将功能房间表示为由房间结构、主要家具到密集支撑面和小物件的分层可执行程序，包含显式几何与功能关系；2) 提出顺序前馈场景构建框架，将递归构建轨迹蒸馏为场景构建专家，推理时逐步编写DSL代码，由确定性执行器直接实例化，无需教师智能体、在线评判或迭代修复；3) 提出ScenePRM，一个基于执行过程的奖励框架，通过强化学习结合功能、几何、关系与未来可构建性反馈来改进专家。

Result: 建立了功能导向基准，在通用室内场景生成和功能房间生成两个任务上都取得SOTA性能，实现了更强的功能完整性、关系正确性、几何可执行性和生成效率。

Conclusion: 通过将功能场景生成转化为可执行程序编写与确定性执行问题，并用执行基础的过程奖励增强专家，可以打破生成-评估-修正循环的瓶颈，实现高效且功能正确的室内场景生成。

Abstract: We introduce Function-Room Generation, a new indoor 3D scene generation setting that creates rooms supporting explicit functional goals rather than merely visually plausible layouts. Existing agentic and executable methods improve controllability, but often depend on costly test-time generate--evaluate--revise loops, making functional room generation slow and computationally expensive. We address this challenge with three technical contributions. First, we design a recursive domain-specific language to effectively organize the hierarchical object compositions required by functional rooms, from room structure and major furniture to dense support-surface and nested small objects. It represents rooms as staged executable programs with explicit geometric and functional relations. Second, we propose a sequential feed-forward scene construction framework that distills recursive construction traces into a scene construction expert. At inference time, the expert writes executable DSL code stage by stage, and a deterministic executor directly instantiates each stage without teacher agents, online critics, or iterative repair. Third, we introduce ScenePRM, an execution-grounded process reward framework that improves the expert through reinforcement learning with functional, geometric, relational, and future-constructability feedback. We further establish a function-oriented benchmark and show state-of-the-art performance on both general indoor scene generation and function-room generation, achieving stronger functional completeness, relation correctness, geometric executability, and generation efficiency.

</details>


### [115] [NepScript Genesis: Neural Architecture Search for Handwritten Devanagari Digit Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29540)
*Mausam Gurung,Prabin Neupane,Sajjan Acharya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NepScript Genesis，一个用于自动发现GAN架构的NAS框架，应用于天城文手写数字的条件生成。对比五种NAS策略，Adaptive Exploration在不到1 GPU小时内将FID从332.28降至79.12（提升76.19%），并在低资源下游任务中通过GAN增强数据将CNN分类准确率从91.0%提升至96.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手写字符合成在低资源场景（如天城文）中具有重要价值，但传统GAN架构设计依赖人工经验且易发生模式崩溃。作者希望利用NAS自动化发现适合特定文字系统的GAN架构，同时引入领域感知的评估指标和脚本特定结构先验来提升生成质量与效率。

Method: 构建DCGAN基线（FID=332.28），设计两阶段NAS流水线，采用五种NAS策略（包括Adaptive Exploration）进行架构搜索。引入新颖的领域感知评估指标Enhanced Score，并在搜索阶段融入脚本特定的结构启发式（如笔画结构先验）以防止早期模式崩溃。最佳模型用于低资源条件下的数据增强，评估其对CNN分类器的影响。

Result: Adaptive Exploration实现最佳质量-效率权衡：FID=79.12（较基线提升76.19%），模式覆盖率最高（Recall=0.531），且在1个GPU小时内完成。脚本特定结构启发式有效防止早期模式崩溃。低资源下游任务中，使用250个真实样本/类加NAS生成数据将CNN分类准确率从91.0%提升至96.5%（+5.5个百分点）。

Conclusion: NAS优化的GAN生成器能产生足够质量的合成手写数字，在真实数据稀缺时可有效提升实际识别管线的性能，验证了自动化架构搜索在领域特定图像合成任务中的价值。

Abstract: This paper introduces NepScript Genesis, a Neural Architecture Search (NAS) framework for automated Generative Adversarial Network (GAN) discovery, applied to conditional Devanagari handwritten digit synthesis. We compare five NAS strategies against a carefully constructed Deep Convolutional GAN (DCGAN) baseline (FID=332.28). Architecture selection utilizes a two-stage pipeline guided by a novel domain-aware evaluation metric (Enhanced Score). Results demonstrate that Adaptive Exploration achieves the optimal quality-efficiency trade-off, attaining an FID of 79.12 -- a 76.19% improvement over the baseline -- and the highest mode coverage among the NAS strategies (Recall=0.531) in under one GPU-hour. Furthermore, we demonstrate that incorporating script-specific structural heuristics into the search phase prevents early-stage mode collapse. In a downstream low-resource evaluation, augmenting 250 real training samples per class with GAN-generated digits from the best NAS model improves CNN classification accuracy from 91.0% to 96.5% (+5.5 percentage points), demonstrating that NAS-optimized synthesis produces digits of sufficient quality to benefit practical recognition pipelines when real data is scarce.

</details>


### [116] [MotionSync: Non-Causal Refinement of Causal Tracker for Label-Efficient 3D Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29567)
*Rahul Ahuja,Bala Murali Manoghar Sai Sudhakar,Shashwata Gupta,Venkatraman Narayanan,Varun Ravi Kumar,Senthil Yogamani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MotionSync将因果/非因果边界作为显式架构接缝，用一个严格因果跟踪器输出在线结果，再通过非因果平滑（RTS平滑、物理验证补全、语义剪枝）修订缓冲轨迹，使一套系统同时服务在线和离线两种范式，无需写回即实现自动标注和跟踪的双重效能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶数据引擎中三维box-track标注是成本瓶颈，现有离线系统完全替代在线感知栈，导致需要同时维护两套系统。作者希望用单一系统同时覆盖在线与离线两套范式，消除重复维护与对账负担。

Method: 以严格因果跟踪器为基础（基于强发表基线），扩展创新驱动的不确定性校准、帧率不变的动力学关联门控、多假设运动与学习模式选择，产生有效在线结果；随后非因果通道用RTS平滑分别应用于位姿、尺寸与偏航角，配合物理验证的间隙补全和基于LiDAR点标签的语义鬼影轨迹剪枝进行修订，且修订结果从不写回因果估计，使精化效果成为对未改动因果估计的增量。

Result: 作为自动标注器，25%人工标签+MotionSync伪标签训练的固定3D检测器在Waymo上达到全监督mAP的96.9%；10%预算下非因果通道比同跟踪器因果阶段的伪标签高出+3.3 mAP/L2。在线跟踪器在其自身精化输出上重新拟合可恢复人工监督益处73%，而因果输出作为监督反而劣于不重新拟合。作为跟踪器，MotionSync在头号指标上与领先离线条目持平，在错误构成上领先，同时减少漏检与碎片化（间隙补全的典型特征）。

Conclusion: MotionSync证明单一系统可以通过显式因果/非因果架构接缝同时服务在线与离线两种范式，非因果精化作为纯增量有效提升自动标注质量，并且精化输出优于因果输出作为监督信号，说明非因果平滑带来的轨迹修正具备真实信息增益。

Abstract: Three-dimensional box-and-track annotation is the cost bottleneck in autonomous-driving data engines, and the offline systems built to relieve it replace the online perception stack outright, so a team needing both regimes maintains and reconciles two. MotionSync makes the causal/non-causal boundary an explicit architectural seam instead. A strictly causal tracker, built on a strong published baseline and extended with innovation-driven uncertainty calibration, frame-rate-invariant kinematic association gates, and multi-hypothesis motion with learned mode selection, emits a valid online result. A non-causal pass then revises the buffered trajectories with Rauch--Tung--Striebel smoothing applied separately to pose, extent and yaw, physics-validated gap completion, and semantic pruning of ghost tracks against LiDAR point labels. The refiner never writes back, so one system serves both regimes and refinement's effect is a delta over an unaltered causal estimate. Used as an auto-labeller, a fixed 3D detector trained on 25% human labels plus MotionSync pseudo-labels reaches 96.9% of its full-supervision mean average precision (mAP) on Waymo, and at a 10% budget the non-causal pass accounts for +3.3 mAP/L2 over pseudo-labels from the same tracker's causal stage. Re-fitting the online tracker on its own refined output recovers 73% of the benefit of human supervision, while its causal output is worse supervision than no re-fitting at all. As a tracker MotionSync is at parity with the leading published offline entries on the headline metric and ahead of them on error composition, which is where a refinement pass can act at all: it reduces misses and fragmentations together, the signature of gap completion rather than of a tuned detector.

</details>


### [117] [TRINITY: A Multi-Perspective Benchmark for Personal-Style Video Highlight Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29577)
*Qianqian Chen,Hyun Bin Kim,Denzel Elden Wijaya,Yang Yi,Bo Liu,Yangkai Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TRINITY基准，将视频高亮检测从单一事件为中心的显著性定义扩展为Event、Emotion、Nature三个互补维度的统一时间框架，并设计了共享骨干+多分支架构进行多视角并行预测，在Mr. HiSum和YouTube Highlights上显著超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频高亮检测依赖狭窄的、以事件为中心的显著性定义，难以泛化到非约束的个人视频，因为这类视频中的高亮是异构且依赖于视角（perspective-dependent）的。

Method: 提出TRINITY基准，将高亮显著性分解为Event（事件）、Emotion（情感）、Nature（自然）三个互补维度，构建统一时间框架；提出共享骨干（shared-backbone）多分支架构，通过视角专用专家（view-specific experts）进行并行多视角预测。

Result: 在Mr. HiSum上取得+7.15/+3.62 mAP（rho=15%/50%）的提升，在YouTube Highlights上取得+10.82 mAP的提升，显著优于SOTA基线。

Conclusion: 多视角建模为视频显著性提供了更稳健、更全面的公式化表达，尤其适用于复杂的真实世界场景。

Abstract: Traditional video highlight detection relies on a narrow, event-centric definition of saliency, which often fails to generalize to unconstrained personal videos where highlights are heterogeneous and perspective-dependent. To address this, we introduce TRINITY, a multi-perspective benchmark that decomposes highlight saliency into three complementary dimensions, Event, Emotion, and Nature, within a unified temporal framework. Leveraging this multi-faceted view, we propose a shared-backbone multi-branch architecture designed for parallel multi-perspective prediction via view-specific experts. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving gains of +7.15/+3.62 mAP (rho=15%/50%) on Mr. HiSum and +10.82 mAP on YouTube Highlights. These results validate that multi-perspective modeling provides a more robust and comprehensive formulation of video saliency, especially for complex real-world scenarios. The benchmark and relevant codes will be released upon acceptance. The benchmark is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/vanilladucky/TRINITY and the code is available at https://github.com/vanilladucky/TRINITY.

</details>


### [118] [Guardrail-Agnostic Societal Bias Evaluation in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29590)
*Yusuke Hirota,Michael Ross Boone,Arun George Zachariah,Jibin Rajan Varghese,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Boyi Li,Ryo Hachiuma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种针对大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）的社会偏见评估方法。传统基准通过询问图像中人物属性（如秘书/CEO）来评估偏见，但强安全护栏模型（GPT、Claude）常拒绝此类提示导致评估不可靠。该方法将任务与人像解耦：不询问人物，而是将图像作为临时用户信息提供人口统计线索，并比较不同人口统计用户下的输出。在故事生成、术语解释、考试问答三个任务上对20个LVLM测试，发现所有模型都在与人物无关的任务中不当地使用人口统计信息（如男性用户故事角色常为机械师，女性为护士）。专有模型（如GPT-5）偏见低于开源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社会偏见基准在强安全护栏的LVLM上会触发拒答，导致评估失效。需要一种能绕过护栏、在模型正常响应前提下可靠测量偏见的新评估范式。

Method: 解耦评估范式：将任务从被描绘人物中分离——使用不询问人物的提示（如"写一个虚构人物故事"），将图像作为"临时用户信息"附加，用以隐式提供人口统计线索，然后比较不同人口统计线索下的模型输出。在故事生成、术语解释和考试式QA三个任务上进行实例化。

Result: 对20个近期LVLM（开源和专有）测试发现：所有模型均在与人物无关的任务中不当地使用用户人口统计信息（如男性用户→机械师角色，女性用户→护士角色）。专有模型（如GPT-5）偏置低于开源模型。

Conclusion: 该解耦评估方法能在护栏模型中可靠测量偏见；所有模型仍存在偏见，但专有模型偏置更低，可能源于持续的模型监控与改进。

Abstract: We propose a societal bias evaluation method for large vision-language models (LVLMs) in the era of strong safety guardrails. Existing benchmarks rely on prompts that ask models to infer attributes of people in images (e.g., "Is this person a CEO or a secretary?"). However, we find that LVLMs with strong guardrails, such as GPT and Claude, often refuse these prompts, making evaluations unreliable. To address this, we change the prior evaluation paradigm by decoupling the task from the depicted person: instead of inferring person's attributes, we use prompts that do not ask about the person (e.g., "Write a fictional story about an imaginary person.") and attach the image as provisional user information to implicitly provide demographic cues, then compare outputs across user demographics. Instantiated across three tasks --- story generation, term explanation, and exam-style QA --- our method avoids refusals even in guardrailed LVLMs, enabling reliable bias measurement. Applying it to 20 recent LVLMs, both open-source and proprietary, we find that all models undesirably use user demographic information in person-irrelevant tasks; for instance, characters in stories are often portrayed as mechanic for male users and nurse for female users. Although still biased, proprietary models like GPT-5 show lower bias than open-source ones. We analyze potential factors behind this gap, discussing continuous model monitoring and improvement as a possible contributor for reducing bias.

</details>


### [119] [SnapBench: Benchmarking Snap-and-Ask Multimodal Retrieval for Mobile Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29607)
*Zirong Chen,Fuda Ye,Kuan Zhang,Enjun Du,Junfu Pu,Xinlei Wang,Xinyu Zuo,Lisheng Duan,Jin Ma,Yongqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SnapBench是首个针对"拍照即问"（snap-and-ask）多模态检索的成对鲁棒性基准测试，涵盖1,145个查询、9,085个图库条目、53种受控退化条件。测试16种多模态检索器，发现图像退化显著降低检索性能，而文本退化影响有限；并提出MOOR（Modality-anchored, Outlier-aware, Optimal Reweighting）自适应融合方法以缓解此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动端AI的snap-and-ask检索已成为常见入口，但现实照片常模糊、文字问题常短或拼写错误。现有基准仅测试干净输入或未隔离snap-and-ask场景中的成对鲁棒性，缺乏对此核心场景的系统评估。

Method: 构建SnapBench基准，包含1,145个查询和9,085个图库条目，在53种受控退化条件下进行人工标注；评估16种多模态检索器（双塔编码器和embedding-based VLM）；提出MOOR自适应融合方法，通过模态锚定、异常值感知和最优重加权实现可靠的模态校准。

Result: 图像退化大幅降低检索性能；文本退化主要影响纯文本检索，对联合检索影响有限；纯图像检索在干净输入下往往优于联合检索，表明存在粗文本拖累和缺乏跨模态回退的问题。MOOR方法展示了可靠性感知模态校准的必要性。

Conclusion: SnapBench提供了snap-and-ask场景下鲁棒检索的受控测试平台，揭示了当前多模态检索器在噪声条件下的脆弱性，并验证了自适应模态融合方法的有效性。

Abstract: Mobile AI acts as a visual oracle, empowering users to snap a picture of something and ask for information. Snap-and-ask retrieval is now one of the most common entry points for mobile AI, yet photos are often blurry, while text questions may be short or mistyped. Existing benchmarks only test on clean inputs or do not isolate paired robustness in snap-and-ask retrieval. Therefore, we introduce SnapBench, the first paired benchmark for robust snap-and-ask multimodal retrieval, spanning 1,145 queries, 9,085 gallery items under 53 controlled corruption conditions with human annotations. We evaluate 16 multimodal retrievers, covering dual-tower encoders and embedding-based VLMs. Results show that image corruptions substantially degrade retrieval, while text corruptions mainly affect text-only retrieval and have limited impact on joint retrieval. Clean image-only retrieval often outperforms joint retrieval, indicating the coarse-text drag and the lack of cross-modal fallback under noisy inputs. SnapBench provides a controlled testbed for evaluating robust retrieval in snap-and-ask scenarios. We further propose MOOR (Modality-anchored, Outlier-aware, Optimal Reweighting), a simple adaptive fusion approach, highlighting the need for reliability-aware modality calibration in snap-and-ask retrieval.

</details>


### [120] [nnMNet: Baseline for Martian Terrain Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29609)
*Ming-Han Lee,Chi-Yeh Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 nnMNet，一个用于火星地形语义分割的新基线模型，基于 nnWNet 引入线性注意力捕获全局上下文、轻量卷积降低计算开销，并设计了空间感知融合块（SAFB）桥接局部与全局表征。作者还构建了统一基准，在 SynMars-TW、SynMars-Air 和 MarsScapes 三个数据集上分别达到 86.61%、83.25% 和 88.24% 的 mIoU，创下新的 SOTA，代码、模型和数据均已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火星地形语义分割对理解这颗最像地球的行星至关重要，但火星表面高度非结构化且复杂，精确的像素级预测和细粒度标注困难。尽管深度学习方法众多，该领域缺乏稳健、公开、可复现的基线和统一基准来进行公平评估。

Method: 在 nnWNet 基础上：(1) 集成线性注意力（linear attention）以更好捕获全局上下文；(2) 使用轻量卷积减少计算开销；(3) 提出空间感知融合块（SAFB），增强并组合不同特征的局部与全局表征；(4) 整理和标准化三个高质量数据集构建统一基准进行公平评估。

Result: nnMNet 在 SynMars-TW、SynMars-Air 和 MarsScapes 上分别达到 86.61%、83.25% 和 88.24% mIoU，均刷新 SOTA。代码、模型和数据集均已公开。

Conclusion: nnMNet 为火星地形语义分割提供了稳健、可复现的新基线和统一基准，通过线性注意力与轻量卷积的融合设计在准确率和效率之间取得了良好平衡，为该领域后续研究奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation is a crucial task for understanding Mars, the most Earth-like planet in our solar system. However, it is challenging because the Martian surface is highly unstructured and complex, making accurate pixel-level prediction and fine-grained annotation difficult. Recent advancements in deep learning have introduced numerous methods and datasets to address these challenges. Nevertheless, the field lacks a robust, publicly available, and reproducible baseline, as well as a unified benchmark to facilitate fair evaluations. In this work, we present nnMNet, a new baseline model designed for Martian terrain semantic segmentation. Building upon nnWNet, we integrate linear attention to better capture global context and employ lightweight convolutions to reduce computational overhead. To bridge the gap between local and global representations, we introduce the Spatially-Aware Fusion Block (SAFB), which augments and combines features with diverse characteristics. Furthermore, we establish a new benchmark by curating and standardizing three high-quality datasets for thorough evaluation. nnMNet achieves new state-of-the-art 86.61%, 83.25%, and 88.24% mIoU on SynMars-TW, SynMars-Air, and MarsScapes, respectively. Our code, models, and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/dereklee0310/nnMNet.

</details>


### [121] [See the Change, Keep the Flow: Unsupervised Action Segmentation via Spectral-Temporal Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29611)
*Yun Li,Jun Xiao,Cong Zhang,Kin-Man Lam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpecT-OT，一种基于谱-时间表示学习框架的无监督动作分割方法。它通过光谱重参数化投影器（SRP）和时域亲和正则化（TAR）两个组件，优化非平衡最优传输伪标签的质量，从而在四个基准上取得SOTA性能，在15项指标中获得13项最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于最优传输（OT）的无监督动作分割方法虽然能提供结构化的帧到动作分配，但伪标签质量严重依赖于构造传输代价所用的表示空间。作者认为可靠的OT伪标签需要表示几何同时具备对判别性动作变化敏感和对局部时间进展连贯的特性。

Method: SpecT-OT包含两个核心组件：(1) Spectral Reparameterization Projector (SRP)——用固定傅里叶基和可学习系数参数化投影器权重，以更好地建模快速变化的判别性特征；(2) Temporal Affinity Regularization (TAR)——对成对帧亲和力施加距离感知、无标签约束，稳定局部时间结构。两者共同产生更具判别性和时间稳定性的传输代价，并通过迭代表示学习生成更可靠的伪标签。

Result: 在四个基准上相比SOTA方法表现出强性能，在15项指标中获得13项最佳结果。在Breakfast上MoF提升4.1点，在Desktop Assembly上F1提升7.4点。

Conclusion: 通过将频谱时间表示学习与最优传输伪标签相结合，可以有效提升无监督动作分割的伪标签可靠性和整体分割精度，验证了在OT框架下显式建模判别性特征和局部时间一致性表示的互补价值。

Abstract: Unsupervised action segmentation aims to discover latent action categories and their temporal organization without action annotations. Optimal transport-based methods provide structured frame-to-action assignments, however, their pseudo-label quality is fundamentally conditioned on the representation space used to construct the transport cost. We argue that reliable OT pseudo-labeling requires a representation geometry that is simultaneously sensitive to discriminative action changes and coherent along local temporal progressions. Based on this insight, we propose SpecT-OT, a spectral-temporal representation learning framework built upon an unbalanced optimal transport pseudo-labeling concept. SpecT-OT introduces a Spectral Reparameterization Projector (SRP), which parameterizes projector weights with fixed Fourier bases and learnable coefficients to improve the modeling of rapidly varying discriminative features, and Temporal Affinity Regularization (TAR), which imposes distance-aware, label-free constraints on pairwise frame affinities to stabilize local temporal structure. The two components jointly produce more discriminative and temporally stable transport costs, yielding more reliable pseudo-labels for iterative representation learning. Experiments on four benchmarks demonstrate strong performance compared with state-of-the-art methods. SpecT-OT achieves the best results on 13 of 15 metrics, including 4.1-point MoF and 7.4-point F1 gains over the baseline on Breakfast and Desktop Assembly, respectively.

</details>


### [122] [CineForge: Self-Improving Agents for Long-Horizon Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29621)
*Junxiang Liu,Lin Wang,Haiyu Shi,Hongxu Ma,Xiaoyu Yang,Chunjie Chen,Xiaoxiao Xu,Kaiqiao Zhan,Boao Wang,Shuizhou Shi,Tianyun Zhu,Jie Li,Jiangtong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CineForge 是一个自进化的视频生成代理框架，由 CineForge-Produce（生成）和 CineForge-Evolve（跨故事策略进化）组成。前者将源故事组织为类型化的叙事/角色/空间/电影状态来协调资产与片段生成，后者通过 Case-to-Pattern-to-Policy Evolution (CPPE) 将反复出现的失败固化为局部策略补丁并部署验证。引入 CineScope 评估套件（100 脚本 + 多尺度指标）。实验表明进化后策略将 CineScope-Metric 从 4.024 提升到 4.380，超过三个长视频基线，并减少 37.0% 的评审 LLM 调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程故事驱动的视频生成需要代理跨多个相互依赖场景协调叙事分解、状态跟踪、镜头设计、提示构建、渲染与修订。现有自适应视频系统仅优化请求或可复用技能，未能将反复出现的生产失败与持久的、分阶段目标的改进相连接，缺乏跨故事的累积式策略进化能力。

Method: 提出 CineForge 框架：(1) CineForge-Produce 将故事解析为类型化状态（叙事、角色、空间、电影）并据此协调资产/片段生成，记录为规范化生产轨迹；(2) CineForge-Evolve 通过 CPPE 流程审查轨迹证据、将反复发现固化为有界的分阶段局部补丁，通过结构重放和置信度控制的配对评估部署更新；(3) 引入 CineScope 评估体系（100 脚本数据集 + 多尺度指标），涵盖因果状态、导演编排、节奏资源分配和角色弧线。

Result: 在 CineScope-Data 和两个公开基准上，进化后的 CineForge 策略将 CineScope-Metric 从 4.024 提升至 4.380，超过三个长视频基线并在 ScriptAgent 下获得一致增益，同时新故事上减少 37.0% 的评审 LLM 调用。

Conclusion: 生产轨迹可以被视为视频代理的可操作经验，使代理能够在长格式叙事任务中累积式改进。该工作确立了跨故事策略进化作为一种有效的自改进范式。

Abstract: Long-horizon story-driven video generation requires a production agent to coordinate narrative decomposition, state tracking, shot design, prompt construction, rendering, and revision across interdependent scenes. Existing adaptive video systems primarily refine requests or reusable skills, leaving recurring production failures disconnected from persistent, stage-targeted improvements across stories. We introduce CineForge, a self-evolving video-production agent framework that couples CineForge-Produce for video generation with CineForge-Evolve for cross-story policy evolution. CineForge-Produce organizes each source story into typed narrative, character, spatial, and cinematic states, uses them to coordinate asset and clip generation, and records the process as a canonical production trajectory. CineForge-Evolve applies Case-to-Pattern-to-Policy Evolution (CPPE) to review trajectory evidence, consolidate recurrent findings into bounded stage-local patches, and deploy validated updates through structural replay and confidence-controlled paired evaluation. To measure complete story realization, we introduce CineScope, which combines a 100-script CineScope-Data suite with a human-aligned, multiscale CineScope-Metric spanning causal state, directorial orchestration, pacing and resource allocation, and character arc. Across CineScope-Data and two public benchmarks, the evolved CineForge policy improves CineScope-Metric from 4.024 to 4.380, outperforms three long-video baselines with consistent gains under ScriptAgent, and reduces review LLM calls by 37.0% on new stories. These results establish production trajectories as actionable experience for video agents that improve cumulatively across long-form storytelling tasks.

</details>


### [123] [SPLG-Mamba: Structure-Preserving Local-Global Mamba Network for Salient Object Detection in Optical Remote Sensing Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29626)
*Yi Xu,Ruichao Hou,Tongwei Ren,Gangshan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SPLG-Mamba网络用于光学遥感图像显著性目标检测，通过平滑-细节重校准、层级感知的局部-全局Mamba和门控跨尺度融合三个模块，解决预测结果中结构退化（碎片化、不完整、前景缺失）问题，在ORSSD、EORSSD和ORSI-4199数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ORSI-SOD方法虽能定位显著区域，但由于层级特征传播中浅层细节引入纹理背景响应、深层语义过度平滑弱结构、以及不受控的跨尺度融合扰动相干区域，导致预测结果出现结构退化（碎片化、不完整、局部前景缺失）。

Method: 提出三模块结构：(1) Smooth-Detail Recalibration (SDR)在状态空间建模前重校准平滑响应与细节残差；(2) hierarchy-aware Local-Global Mamba在浅层特征用局部建模、深层特征用全局建模；(3) Gated Cross-Scale Fusion (GCSF)在解码时控制跨尺度细节注入。

Result: 在ORSSD、EORSSD和ORSI-4199三个公开数据集上达到state-of-the-art效果，提升了结构完整性和连续性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: SPLG-Mamba通过有针对性的结构保持设计有效缓解了ORSI-SOD中的结构退化问题，验证了Mamba架构在遥感显著性检测中的潜力。

Abstract: Salient object detection in optical remote sensing images (ORSI-SOD) requires dense predictions that preserve object completeness and structural continuity under complex backgrounds, scale variation, and irregular object shapes. Existing methods often localize salient regions, but their predictions may still suffer from structural degradation, including fragmented, incomplete, or locally missing foreground responses. This degradation is closely related to hierarchical feature propagation, where shallow details can introduce texture-induced background responses, deep semantics may over-smooth weak structures, and uncontrolled cross-scale fusion can disturb coherent regions. To address this issue, we propose a novel Structure-Preserving Local-Global Mamba Network, SPLG-Mamba, for ORSI-SOD. Specifically, SPLG-Mamba integrates Smooth-Detail Recalibration (SDR), hierarchy-aware Local-Global Mamba, and Gated Cross-Scale Fusion (GCSF). SDR recalibrates smoothed responses and detail residuals before state-space modeling, Local-Global Mamba assigns local modeling to shallow feature levels and global modeling to deep feature levels, and GCSF controls cross-scale detail injection during decoding. Experiments on ORSSD, EORSSD, and ORSI-4199 demonstrate state-of-the-art results and improved structural completeness and continuity. The code is available at https://github.com/yxu9910/SPLG-Mamba

</details>


### [124] [Conducting Stylistic Analysis of Paintings through an Art-History Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29644)
*Marc S. Walton,Astrid Harth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个AI框架，通过视觉Transformer在大规模绘画语料上训练并编码艺术史特征，结合稀疏字典学习分解为可共享的视觉特征，再由大语言模型将这些特征翻译为描述性术语，最后通过ReAct框架的自主协调LLM对特征进行加权、测试和精炼，实现绘画的风格化分析自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统艺术史的风格分析依赖于人工视觉观察与描述，而当前AI模型仅提供无法解释的概率分类，存在方法论鸿沟。作者希望建立一种可解释的、能产出描述性术语的AI框架，弥合图像数据计算与人文语义关切之间的差距。

Method: 1) 用ViT在带元数据的大规模绘画语料上训练，将艺术史数据编码为嵌入；2) 通过稀疏字典学习将嵌入分解为一组在训练集中反复出现的共享特征；3) 用LLM检索与每个特征关联的作品及其策展人撰写的文本，综合生成反映风格属性的描述；4) 用ReAct框架的自主协调LLM对特征进行加权、测试和精炼，生成连贯的作品描述或作品比较。

Result: 该方法能将详细的视觉特征转化为描述性术语，实现了绘画风格分析的自动化，并支撑证据收集、发现和验证，为基于视觉的计算艺术史领域奠定基础。

Conclusion: 该框架成功弥合了艺术史传统风格分析与当代AI模型之间的方法论鸿沟，将图像作为数据与人文语义关切相连接，确立了视觉计算艺术史作为未来增长领域的方向。

Abstract: Attributing an artwork to an artist has traditionally relied on detailed visual observations and descriptions, known as stylistic analysis in art history. By contrast, current artificial intelligence (AI) models used in the field offer only unexplained probabilistic classifications. To bridge this methodological gap, we present an AI framework that automates stylistic analysis of paintings, providing a foundation for enhancing evidence collection, discovery, and verification. By training a vision transformer (ViT) on a large corpus of paintings with metadata, our system encodes this art history-specific data as embeddings. These representations are factorized via sparse dictionary learning into a shared set of features that recur across the training set. A large language model (LLM) then interprets each feature by retrieving associated artworks and their accompanying curator-written texts, and synthesizes them into descriptions that reflect their stylistic attributes. Finally, an autonomous coordinator LLM applies a reasoning-and-action (ReAct) framework to weight, test, and refine these features into cohesive descriptions of an artwork, or comparisons of artworks. This approach converts detailed visual features into descriptive terms, addressing a key challenge in art history. It thus connects the use of images as data with the semantic concerns of humanists, establishing vision-based computational art history as an area for future growth.

</details>


### [125] [PhysVR: Vision-Language Model Guided Interference-aware Temporal Feature Refinement for Remote Physiological Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29663)
*Zixu Li,Jianjun Qian,Hang Shao,Daoheng Li,Lei Luo,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysVR，一个由视觉-语言模型(VLM)引导的干扰感知时序特征精炼框架，用于远程光电容积描记(rPPG)估计。该方法通过构建信号衍生的生理可靠性证据和VLM提取的视觉干扰证据，利用时间交叉注意力整合干扰感知上下文，并通过共享时序校正单元与四个干扰特异专家自适应地抑制光照变化、头部运动、面部模糊和ROI不稳定等干扰。在五个公开基准上的实验表明PhysVR在数据集内和跨数据集评估协议下均持续优于代表性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有rPPG方法主要在特征学习过程中抑制干扰，但很少检查学到的时序特征是否仍受干扰影响，且缺乏在rPPG估计前进一步抑制干扰的手段。作者旨在引入VLM的语义理解能力来显式识别和抑制各类干扰源，弥补现有方法忽视特征层面干扰残留的不足。

Method: PhysVR包含几个关键组件：(1) 生理骨干网络生成全局时序特征和粗略rPPG预测，并从局部时序特征构建信号衍生的生理可靠性证据；(2) 冻结的视觉-语言模型在干扰导向提示下处理采样的人脸帧，证据头提取视觉干扰证据；(3) 时间交叉注意力融合生理与视觉证据与全局时序特征，构建干扰感知时序上下文；(4) 共享时序校正单元进行通用精炼，四个干扰特异专家通过自适应路由选择性地抑制不同干扰；(5) 精炼后的时序特征用于最终rPPG估计。

Result: 在五个公开基准上，PhysVR在数据集内和跨数据集两种评估协议下均一致优于现有代表性rPPG方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入VLM语义理解能力来构建干扰感知的时序特征精炼机制，PhysVR有效提升了rPPG估计在复杂干扰条件下的鲁棒性，验证了"估计前先精炼"这一策略的有效性。

Abstract: Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) enables contactless physiological measurement from facial videos, yet its subtle pulse-related variations are easily affected by illumination variation, head motion, facial blur, and region-of-interest instability. Existing methods mainly suppress interference during feature learning, while whether the learned temporal features remain affected by interference and how to further suppress such interference before rPPG estimation are rarely examined. To address this limitation, we propose PhysVR, a vision-language model guided interference-aware temporal feature refinement framework for rPPG estimation. Specifically, a physiological backbone produces global temporal features and a coarse rPPG prediction, from which signal-derived physiological reliability evidence is constructed from local temporal characteristics. In parallel, a frozen vision-language model processes sampled facial frames under an interference-oriented prompt, and an evidence head extracts visual interference evidence from the VLM output. Temporal cross-attention integrates the physiological and visual evidence with the global temporal features to construct interference-aware temporal context. Guided by this context, a shared temporal correction unit performs general refinement, while four interference-specific experts selectively suppress different interference through adaptive routing. The refined temporal features are then used for final rPPG estimation. Extensive experiments on five public benchmarks demonstrate that PhysVR consistently outperforms representative methods under both intra-dataset and cross-dataset evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [126] [MedSegBenchmarker: A Raw-Count-First Framework for Controlled 2D Medical Image Segmentation Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29677)
*Vanessa Borst,Lukas Horn,Daniel Grillmeyer,Thomas Prantl,Samuel Kounev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了MEDSEGBENCHMARKER (MSB)，一个配置驱动的医学图像分割（2D MIS）可控基准测试框架，整合重复图像检测、分组数据划分、YAML研究配置、可恢复训练、超参数优化、交叉验证和基于检查点的评估，并导出样本/类别级像素计数与预测以支持事后分析。案例研究表明评估与实验设置中的细微选择会影响基准结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割中不同数据集、评估协议和快速演进的架构使得公平可复现的模型比较十分困难，现有比较通常隐含假设模型排名在不同数据划分、预处理、指标聚合和计算约束下保持不变，缺乏可扩展的统一评估框架。

Method: MSB框架由配置驱动，包含重复/近重复图像检测、分组感知数据划分、YAML研究规格说明、可恢复训练、超参数优化、交叉验证和基于检查点的评估。而非仅保留聚合性能指标，MSB导出样本级和类别级像素计数与预测及评估上下文，实现无需重复推理的事后分析。

Result: 在三个异质2D数据集、多个MIS与通用视觉模型、256与512像素分辨率的案例研究中，重新聚合相同预测在六个数据集-分辨率设置中的三个改变了排名榜首的架构，尽管聚合策略间秩相关很高。提高输入分辨率产生模型和数据集相关的性能增减，需结合经验测量的推理复杂度一起考虑。

Conclusion: 评估和实验设置中看似微小的选择可能影响基准结论。MSB提供了实用且可扩展的基础，使基准条件和评估选择明确且可复现，代码已在GitHub开放。

Abstract: Despite rapid advances in MIS, fair and reproducible comparisons of segmentation models remain challenging due to heterogeneous datasets, inconsistent evaluation protocols, and rapidly evolving architectures. In particular, comparisons often implicitly assume that model rankings are invariant to data partitioning, preprocessing, metric aggregation, uncertainty estimation, and computational constraints. The lack of extensible and unified evaluation frameworks further limits systematic investigation of new models, datasets, and training paradigms. We present MEDSEGBENCHMARKER (MSB), a configuration-driven framework for controlled benchmarking of 2D MIS. It integrates duplicate and near-duplicate image detection, group-aware data splitting, YAML study specifications, resumable training, hyperparameter optimization, cross-validation, and checkpoint-based evaluation. Rather than retaining only aggregate performance measures, MSB exports sample- and class-level pixel counts and predictions together with the evaluation context. These elementary artifacts enable post-hoc analyses without repeated inference. We demonstrate MSB in a case study involving three heterogeneous 2D datasets and multiple MIS and general-purpose vision models evaluated at 256- and 512-pixel input resolutions. Reaggregation of identical predictions changes the top-ranked architecture in three of six dataset-resolution settings, despite high rank correlations between aggregation strategies. Increasing input resolution produces model- and dataset-dependent performance gains and losses that must be considered alongside empirically measured inference complexity. These results show that seemingly minor choices in evaluation and experimental setup can affect benchmark conclusions. MSB, available at GitHub, provides a practical and extensible basis for making benchmark conditions and evaluation choices explicit and reproducible.

</details>


### [127] [GeoRay: Gauge-Aware Feed-Forward Satellite 3D Reconstruction in the Geodetic Frame](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29680)
*Zhe Dong,Wanqing Wu,Yuzhe Sun,Haochen Jiang,Yuchen Ma,Lecheng Ren,Tianzhu Liu,Yanfeng Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoRay方法，解决卫星摄影测量在绝对大地坐标框架下的稠密地表高程重建问题。通过轻量射线适配器使冻结的透视预训练骨干网络适配非中心RPC射线，显式基准(datum)机制分离地形起伏与绝对高程，并采用校准逆方差融合结合单目与多视角两种高程流。在18个系统/26个US3D瓦片上的绝对基准中达到2.99m绝对MAE、91.9%覆盖率，比最强合规前馈基线提升46.4点，单瓦片推理仅需24秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈3D基础模型擅长透视场景的一次性重建，但卫星摄影测量需要不同的产品：在绝对大地坐标下、使用非中心有理多项式相机(RPC)恢复稠密地表高程。现有透视预训练特征在RPC高度射线上不可靠，绝对高程存在与传感器偏差耦合的低阶基准不确定性问题，且单目与多视角线索在不同区域各自失效。这些问题无法仅靠域自适应解决，需要专门的方法设计。

Method: (1) 轻量射线一致适配器：使冻结的透视预训练骨干沿原生RPC射线可匹配；(2) 显式基准(datum)机制：分离地形起伏(relief)与绝对高程层级，对垂直原点具有等变性，一个训练模型可服务零控制、稀疏控制和单控制推理；(3) 校准逆方差融合：结合单目与多视角两种高程流。并构建绝对框架基准，不依赖配准或测试参考泄漏。

Result: 在26个US3D留出瓦片上，GeoRay达到2.99m绝对MAE、91.9%覆盖率；在完整性感知精度上比最强合规前馈基线提升46.4个百分点；在跨数据集和跨城市两种迁移场景下仍保持最准确；单瓦片模型前向推理仅需24秒。代码和模型将开源。

Conclusion: 通过射线适配、显式基准机制和双流融合三个针对性设计，GeoRay在绝对高程重建上显著超越了直接域自适应的前馈基线，同时支持零/稀疏/单控制推理的灵活部署，具备实时推理能力和开源可复现性，是该领域的强基线。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D foundation models reconstruct perspective scenes in one pass. Satellite photogrammetry needs a different product, one that domain adaptation alone does not deliver: dense surface height in an absolute geodetic frame under non-central rational polynomial cameras (RPCs). Perspective-pretrained features are not reliably observable along RPC height rays, absolute elevation carries a low-order height--datum gauge exchangeable with sensor bias to first order, and monocular and multi-view cues fail in different regions. \method{} treats all three. Lightweight ray-consistent adapters make a frozen backbone matchable along native RPC rays. An explicit datum mechanism separates relief from absolute level and is equivariant to the vertical origin by construction, so one trained model serves zero-, one-, and sparse-control inference. Calibrated inverse-variance fusion combines the two relief streams. \bench{}, our absolute-frame benchmark of eighteen systems across in-domain, cross-dataset, and cross-city tiers, scores absolute placement without registration or test-reference leakage. On 26 held-out US3D tiles, \method{} attains $2.99$\,m absolute MAE at $91.9\%$ coverage, improves completeness-aware accuracy by $46.4$ points over the strongest compliant feed-forward baseline, remains the most accurate such system under both transfer shifts, and runs in $24$\,s model-forward time per tile. Code and models will be released at https://github.com/HIT-SIRS/GeoRay

</details>


### [128] [A Calibration Audit of Confidence in Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29705)
*Nanxing Nick Deng,Qing Cheng,Niclas Zeller,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文审计了七个前馈3D重建模型的逐像素置信度，评估其作为不确定性信号的四项性质（误差排序、层级准确、跨置信范围一致性、区间覆盖）。发现置信度排序误差能力良好，但预测的不确定性系统性偏低（中位数误差2.4x），且越自信偏差越大。提出双常数幂律校准可将中位数误差降至1.35x，少量标注场景重拟合可达1.12x，但无法修正逐场景的形状误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈3D重建模型输出的逐像素置信度被下游系统当作可靠性信号读取，但其训练目标仅是损失权重而非不确定性幅值，其能否作为误差预测从未被系统度量。本文旨在审计这些置信度信号的可靠性，填补该空白。

Method: 在13个数据集上审计7个已发布的前馈重建骨干网络，从四个性质评估置信度：误差排序质量、平均层级准确性、跨置信范围一致性、区间覆盖真相。提出每个骨干-数据集配两个常数的幂律校准函数，并分析训练损失最优与过置信现象共存的原因；尝试多种重缩放方法以修正场景级偏差。

Result: 置信度的误差排序良好，但在训练分布外条件下预测不确定性系统性偏低，中位误差为2.4x，且模型越自信误差预测越差。该现象在损失达到最优时仍可出现。幂律校准可将中位数误差降至1.35x（外推）和1.12x（少量场景重拟合），但没有任何重缩放能修正场景级形状误差——场景缺少的是形状而非平移，三分之二的留出场景仍偏离5个百分点。

Conclusion: 前馈3D重建模型的置信度在排序上可靠但幅值系统性低估，可用简单的每模型-数据集双常数幂律校准改善总体幅值，但场景级不确定性形态误差本质源于模型缺乏跨预测的尺度知识，无法通过标量重缩放解决。作者发布审计协议、结果和拟合常数。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction models emit a per-pixel confidence that downstream systems read as a reliability signal. It is trained as a loss weight, not as an uncertainty magnitude, and whether it can be used as an error prediction has not been measured. We audit seven released backbones on thirteen datasets and score the confidence on four properties, how well it ranks error, whether its level is right on average, whether it holds across the confidence range, and whether its intervals cover the truth. The confidence ranks error well, but the predicted uncertainty is too low when it is read under conditions that are not exactly those of training. The median case is off by 2.4x across all seven models, and the error prediction is further off the more confident the model is. We show that this phenomenon can appear even though the loss's optimum is reached. A released model resumed under its own loss reaches that optimum on its training data within a few hundred updates and stays overconfident on unseen frames. A power law with two constants per backbone and dataset corrects the overall magnitude of the predicted uncertainty and leaves the ranking untouched. What no rescaling reaches is the scene, which we attribute to the model's missing knowledge of scale across predictions. Every correction we tried is close to right on average and still leaves two thirds of held-out scenes outside a five-point band, because what a scene is missing is a shape rather than a shift. We release the audit protocol, its results, and the fitted constants per model and dataset. Fitted with the target dataset held out, the constants bring the median case from 2.4x off to 1.35x, and a refit on a few labelled scenes of that dataset reaches 1.12x.

</details>


### [129] [XDG: Accelerated Visual Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29733)
*Gonglin Chen,Ben Southall,Hanyuan Xiao,Wenbin Teng,Haolin Xiong,Tianwen Fu,Junyi Ouyang,Kshitij Singh Minhas,Supun Samarasekera,Rakesh Kumar,Yajie Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: XDG是一个高效的视觉重影/别名消歧模型，通过微调Depth Anything 3基础模型（使用轻量LoRA适配器并复用其相机token作为分类token）来判断图像对是否观测同一3D表面，相比现有方法在保持竞争力的同时实现超过3倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构从运动（SfM）中视觉别名（doppelganger问题）会导致错误图像匹配并降低重建质量。现有方法使用几何感知基础模型特征但在骨干网络上叠加重型transformer分类器，使大规模消歧计算昂贵。目标是构建高效且可扩展的视觉消歧模型。

Method: 关键观察：3D基础模型本身已具备跨视图几何推理能力，因此消歧分类应直接适配骨干表示而非在单独的重型解码器中重新学习成对推理。XDG微调Depth Anything 3，使用轻量LoRA适配器，并复用其相机token作为紧凑的成对分类token，通过一个紧凑的MLP头预测图像对是否观测同一3D表面。

Result: 在成对和重建基准上，XDG与最先进的消歧方法保持竞争力，同时提供超过3倍的推理加速。在包含数千张图像的单个LaMAR场景上，XDG节省超过10小时的视觉消歧处理时间。

Conclusion: XDG证明了3D基础模型的结构化表示可以被直接适配用于高效视觉消歧，在保持精度的同时大幅提升推理效率，为大规模SfM消歧提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Visual aliasing, also known as the doppelganger problem, remains a key challenge for structure-from-motion (SfM): visually similar but physically distinct surfaces can produce incorrect image matches and degrade reconstruction quality. Previous work mitigates this issue with geometry-aware foundation-model features, but places a heavy transformer classifier on top of the backbone, making large-scale disambiguation expensive. We introduce XDG, an efficient visual disambiguation model designed for scalable SfM. Our key observation is that a 3D foundation model already performs the cross-view geometric reasoning necessary for visual disambiguation, so doppelganger classification should adapt the backbone representation directly rather than relearn pair reasoning in a separate heavy decoder. XDG fine-tunes Depth Anything 3 with lightweight LoRA adapters and repurposes its camera tokens as compact pair-level classification tokens. A compact MLP head predicts whether a candidate image pair observes the same 3D surface. Extensive experiments show that XDG provides a favorable accuracy-efficiency tradeoff: it remains competitive with the state-of-the-art disambiguation method across pairwise and reconstruction benchmarks and delivers more than a 3x inference speedup. On individual LaMAR scenes containing thousands of images, XDG saves more than 10 hours of visual disambiguation processing. Code is available at https://github.com/xtcpete/xdg.

</details>


### [130] [Drift Calibration in Geometric Eye Tracking Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29739)
*Jiaqi Liu,Zixuan Wang,Yuhong Zhang,Dingkang Liang,Jane Hanqi Li,Tzyy-Ping Jung,Gert Cauwenberghs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个专注于校准的注视点追踪数据集（163次试验，12名参与者，18点拟合网格和32点测试网格），并在统一的空间外推协议下评估了全局、局部和组合校正函数。此外引入了一个轻量级神经精细化器，组合互补校准器的排序预测。在受控数据集上，后厂商校正将平均角度误差从1.53°降至1.03°（最强经典组合）和0.96°（精细化器）。在闭环注视任务中，较低残余误差与较高性能相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何眼动追踪器虽然能提供凝视交互所需的精度，但仍对会话特定的残留校准误差敏感。现有校正方法的研究难以比较，因为不同方法通常使用不同的设备、目标布局和误差定义进行评估。需要一个统一的基准来公平评估和促进该领域研究。

Method: 构建了包含18点拟合网格和32点测试网格的校准数据集，在统一的空间外推协议下评估全局、局部和组合校正函数，并提出轻量级神经精细化器来组合互补校准器的排序预测。

Result: 后厂商校正将平均角度误差从1.53°降至1.03°（最强经典组合校正），神经网络精细化器进一步降至0.96°。在闭环凝视任务中，四种在线校正条件下较低残余误差与更高性能相关。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可复现的数据质量基准，用于将凝视作为交互建模中的行为信号，并验证了组合校正与神经精细化方法在降低校准误差方面的有效性。

Abstract: Geometric eye trackers can provide the spatial accuracy required for gaze-based interaction and multimodal studies, but their measurements remain sensitive to residual session-specific calibration error. Research on correcting this error is difficult to compare because methods are typically evaluated with different devices, target layouts, and error definitions. We present a calibration-focused dataset containing 163 trials from 12 participants, with separate 18-point fitting and 32-point test grids, and use it to evaluate global, local, and composite correction functions under a common spatial-extrapolation protocol. We further introduce a lightweight neural refiner that combines ranked predictions from complementary calibrators. On this controlled dataset, post-vendor correction reduces the mean angular error from $1.53^\circ$ to $1.03^\circ$ with the strongest classical composite and to $0.96^\circ$ with the refiner. In a closed-loop gaze task, lower residual error is associated with higher performance across four online correction conditions. These results provide a reproducible data-quality benchmark for using gaze as a behavioral signal in interactive modeling.

</details>


### [131] [SynCrash: A Multi-Stage Pipeline for Zero-Shot Accident Detection and Localization in Traffic Surveillance Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29759)
*Arkya Jyoti Bagchi,Ritul Jangir,Varun Raskar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SynCrash是一个用于固定视角CCTV监控视频中零样本事故检测、空间定位和碰撞类型分类的多阶段流水线，在CVPR 2026 ACCIDENT挑战赛中使用。它通过三个解耦阶段工作：基于VideoMAEv2-giant在CARLA合成数据上微调的时间定位、基于YOLO检测器与物理启发式混合算法的空间定位，以及基于车辆数量与构型的轻量级规则式碰撞分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决真实世界中事故检测缺少标注数据的挑战——挑战要求在无真实标注训练数据的情况下同时预测事故发生时间、帧内冲击位置和碰撞类型，因此需要利用合成数据和域无关先验实现零样本泛化。

Method: 三段解耦流水线：(1)时间定位采用VideoMAEv2-giant骨干网络，在CARLA合成片段上微调，使用元数据感知嵌入和密集滑动窗口推理；(2)空间定位使用YOLO目标检测结合物理信息混合启发式，基于边界框重叠和轨迹推理预测冲击点；(3)碰撞类型分类使用基于检测车辆数量和构型的规则策略。

Result: 论文以摘要形式呈现，未在摘要中给出定量结果数据，但表明该方法在CVPR 2026 ACCIDENT挑战赛中被采纳，核心思想是时间理解受益于合成数据的监督微调，而空间理解更适合预训练检测器与物理先验。

Conclusion: 关键洞见是任务解耦与领域自适应策略差异化：时间维度通过合成数据微调获得强监督信号，空间维度依赖跨域迁移的物理先验和预训练检测器，两者协同实现零样本事故检测。

Abstract: We present SynCrash, a multi-stage pipeline for zero-shot accident detection, spatial localization, and collision-type classification in fixed-view CCTV surveillance video. Our approach addresses the ACCIDENT at CVPR 2026 Challenge, which requires predicting when an accident occurs, where in the frame the impact happens, and what type of collision it is, all without access to labeled real-world training data. The pipeline operates in three decoupled stages: (1) Temporal localization via a VideoMAEv2-giant backbone fine-tuned on CARLA-based synthetic clips with metadata-aware embeddings and dense sliding-window inference; (2) Spatial localization using YOLO for object detection combined with a physics-informed hybrid heuristic that leverages bounding-box overlap and trajectory-based reasoning to predict the impact point; and (3) Collision-type classification using a lightweight rule-based strategy derived from the number and configuration of detected vehicles. The key insight is that temporal understanding benefits from supervised fine-tuning on synthetic data, whereas spatial understanding is better served by pretrained object detectors and physics priors that transfer naturally across domains.

</details>


### [132] [InspectorGPT: A Comparative Reasoning Enhanced VLM for Comprehensive Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29783)
*Weifei Chen,Honghao Zhang,Zhiyuan You,Xinyi Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出InspectorGPT，一个基于对比推理的VLM框架，用于工业异常检测。通过将查询图像与无缺陷参考图像进行比较推理，结合CoT微调和GRPO强化学习实现多任务检测；并引入InspectorGPT-Seg通过任务向量融合实现像素级分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业异常检测方法存在三方面不足：传统无监督方法泛化性差；引入VLM的零样本方法在推理导向的后训练中会出现异常判别能力崩溃；现有方法缺乏像素级分割能力且缺乏人类式的"对比参考"检测原则。

Method: 提出InspectorGPT，核心思想是模拟人类检测员将查询图像与正常参考图像进行对比推理。通过CoT微调将对比推理能力内化到VLM中，并使用GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）配合可验证奖励进行强化学习优化。对于像素级分割，引入InspectorGPT-Seg分支，通过任务向量融合将分割分支与推理分支结合，避免联合训练导致的能力失衡。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法在多维度性能上达到领先水平，并在未见基准上表现良好泛化性，验证了对比推理对综合工业检测的有效性。

Conclusion: 对比推理范式是工业异常检测的有效原则，能够兼顾细粒度分割和语义推理，且具有良好的跨类别泛化能力。任务向量融合是解决多任务能力失衡的有效手段。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection is a critical component of modern manufacturing. Most traditional unsupervised methods rely on modelling normal feature distributions, inherently limiting generalization to unknown categories. To improve generalizability, some recent methods incorporate vision-language models (VLMs) for zero-shot detection via text prompts. However, we observe that reasoning-oriented post-training can cause anomaly discrimination to collapse, with some fine-tuned models performing worse than their base VLMs. Existing methods also provide only textual decisions or coarse boxes, without pixel-level segmentation. A more explicit detection principle comes from human inspection: anomalies are identified by comparing a query image with a defect-free reference. Inspired by this, we propose InspectorGPT, a VLM framework centered on comparative reasoning. Given a normal reference and a query image, InspectorGPT compares them to identify discrepancies and perform multiple inspection tasks with detailed reasoning. We internalize this capability through Chain-of-Thought (CoT) fine-tuning and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with tailored, verifiable rewards. We further introduce InspectorGPT-Seg for pixel-level anomaly masks. Segmentation supervision improves anomaly discrimination but weakens semantic reasoning, while joint training fails to balance them. We therefore train the two branches separately and combine them through task-vector fusion. Extensive experiments demonstrate superior multi-dimensional performance and generalization to unseen benchmarks, validating comparative reasoning for comprehensive industrial inspection.

</details>


### [133] [GridFlow: Structured Latent Flow for Seamless City-Scale 3D Point Cloud Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29793)
*Xinyu Wang,Muhammad Ibrahim,Atif Mansoor,Ajmal Mian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个多阶段框架City3D-MultiGen，用于从卫星影像、语义分割图和DSM生成城市尺度的稠密彩色点云，实现无缝拼接的大规模3D城市环境生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的点云生成方法局限于单物体或有界室内场景，无法应对城市尺度生成所面临的大规模、无缝拼接和部分可观测性挑战。生成真实的3D城市环境对仿真、城市规划、混合现实等领域至关重要。

Method: 多阶段框架：(1) Grid-Aligned VAE将每个瓦片编码为保拓扑的潜在网格，token对应固定空间区域，实现多模态条件注入和紧凑的潜在空间边缘一致性（隐式对齐数千个边界点）；(2) 条件整流流模型从融合的多模态条件合成几何潜在表示；(3) 面向朝向的扩散彩色化器分别处理卫星可见的水平表面和遮挡的垂直立面。引入City3D-MultiGen基准（163K标注瓦片，来自墨尔本和伦敦）。

Result: 在所有几何指标上优于适配的现有点云生成基线，生成视觉连贯的彩色点云，能在任意大的城市范围内实现无缝边界拼接。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了城市尺度点云生成的三大挑战（规模、无缝拼接、部分可观测性），并提供了标准化评估基准。

Abstract: Generating realistic 3D city environments from remote sensing data is important for simulation, urban planning, and mixed reality, yet existing point cloud generation methods are limited to single objects or bounded indoor scenes and cannot handle the scale, seamless tiling, and partial observability challenges of city-scale generation. We present \ours{}, a multi-stage framework that generates dense, colored point clouds ($10^5$ points per $150\text{m}{\times}150\text{m}$ tile) at city scale, conditioned on satellite imagery, semantic segmentation maps, and digital surface models (DSM). A \emph{Grid-Aligned VAE} encodes each tile into a topology-preserving latent grid where tokens correspond to fixed spatial regions, enabling spatially coherent multi-modal conditioning and compact latent-space edge consistency that implicitly aligns thousands of boundary points for seamless cross-tile generation. A conditional rectified flow model synthesizes geometry latents from the fused multi-modal conditions, and an orientation-aware diffusion colorizer separately handles satellite-visible horizontal surfaces and occluded vertical façades. To support standardized evaluation, we build on public 3D data sources to introduce \emph{City3D-MultiGen}, a benchmark of $163$K densely annotated tiles from Melbourne and London with aligned point clouds, satellite images, semantic maps, and elevation data. Experiments show that \ours{} outperforms adapted point cloud generation baselines across all geometry metrics and produces visually coherent colored point clouds with seamless boundaries over arbitrarily large urban extents. Our benchmark details are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/e32/City3D-MultiGen

</details>


### [134] [Source-Dependent Deference in Medical Imaging Agents Under Falsified Findings: A Pilot Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29800)
*Ridam Roy,Md Shahriar Rashid,Md. Rajib Mia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究审计了ReAct风格的工具调用agent在医学影像问答中的行为：当工具返回虚假(否定)发现时，agent是否会放弃原本正确的答案，以及该行为是否取决于信息的呈现方式。在20个VQA-RAD闭卷问题上，以散文形式归因于放射科医生的否定发现会引发SOTA模型在13例中修订10例正确答案，而以JSON工具形式呈现时仅13例中1例修订（精确McNemar p=0.0039）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具调用agent正被用于医学影像领域，但其在工具返回虚假发现时的行为几乎未被测量。理解agent对错误工具输出的服从/信任程度，以及呈现方式的影响，对安全部署至关重要。

Method: 在20个VQA-RAD闭卷问题上，跨四个厂商指定的模型层级，让ReAct风格agent先仅凭影像作答；随后通过两种渠道交付否定发现：(1) 由agent自己调用的analyze_image工具返回的JSON；(2) 归因于放射科医生的引用散文。衡量在无工具时正确回答的情况下agent修正答案的commission-error率。

Result: 散文归因形式的服从度远高于工具JSON形式：最强模型层级在13个正确案例中10例修订答案（散文）对比1例（工具），精确McNemar p=0.0039（Holm校正后0.012）。作者未声称这隔离了来源标签效应，因为来源与传递渠道在设计上纠缠，且工具声明仅在agent主动调用工具时才到达agent。属小规模初步研究，预设停止规则未达标。

Conclusion: 发现了一个联合的来源与传递渠道不对称效应：agent对归因于放射科医生的散文声明比对自己调用的JSON工具输出表现出显著更高的服从度。结果来自小规模试点，需谨慎解释，但提示呈现方式显著影响工具调用agent对不可靠信息的采纳。

Abstract: Tool-using agents are being proposed for medical imaging, and their behaviour when a tool returns a false finding is largely unmeasured. We audit whether a ReAct-style tool-calling agent abandons an answer it has already given correctly once a falsified finding arrives, and whether that depends on how the finding is presented. On 20 VQA-RAD closed questions across four vendor-designated model tiers, the agent commits to an answer from the image alone; a negated finding is then delivered either as JSON from an analyze_image tool the agent invokes itself, or as quoted prose attributed to a radiologist. Our outcome is the commission-error rate over cases answered correctly without any tool. Deference is much higher under the prose-attributed claim: at the strongest tier the agent revised its correct answer in 10 of 13 cases against 1 of 13 under the tool (exact McNemar p=0.0039, Holm-adjusted 0.012). We do not claim this isolates the source label. Attribution travels with the delivery channel in our design, and exposure differs because the tool claim reaches the agent only when it calls the tool. The finding is a joint source-and-delivery asymmetry from a small-scale pilot whose pre-specified stopping rule was not met.

</details>


### [135] [Foundation and Multimodal Large Language Models for Face Presentation and Morph Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29802)
*Hatef Otroshi Shahreza,Asif Hussain Khan,Peter Lorenz,Alain Komaty,Sébastien Marcel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了通用基础模型（FMs）和多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在人脸演示攻击检测（PAD）和人脸变形攻击检测（MAD）中的表现，提出了五种利用模型内部信息的部署策略，并构建了PADLLM和MADLLM两个专用多模态大模型。在4个PAD数据集和4个MAD数据集上对16个MLLM和30个视觉编码器进行了跨数据集基准评测，微调模型达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸识别系统在安全关键应用中广泛部署，但仍易受演示攻击和变形攻击。现有PAD/MAD检测器泛化能力有限，在跨数据集评估中性能退化。作者希望探究通用基础模型和多模态大模型是否编码了攻击相关信息，以及如何最佳部署这些模型用于两项任务。

Method: 提出五种由浅入深的模型部署策略：(i) 零样本提示MLLM；(ii) 在MLLM输出的next-token logits上训练浅层模型；(iii) 在任务特定问答数据上做参数高效微调，得到PADLLM和MADLLM并提供文本推理；(iv) 对冻结视觉编码器做线性探测；(v) 微调FM和MLLM的视觉编码器。在16个MLLM和30个视觉编码器上进行系统评测。

Result: 实验表明FMs和MLLMs在PAD和MAD任务上能达到显著性能。微调模型在跨数据集评估中取得最先进检测性能，证明通用预训练表征携带大量攻击相关信息。

Conclusion: 通用基础模型预训练表征中蕴含丰富的攻击相关信息，通过适当微调策略可有效部署于PAD和MAD任务，优于现有专用检测器。源代码将公开。

Abstract: Face recognition systems are increasingly deployed in security-critical applications, yet they remain vulnerable to presentation and morph attacks. Presentation attack detection (PAD) and morphing attack detection (MAD) are therefore essential components of trustworthy face biometrics. Despite advancements in PAD and MAD methods, existing detectors suffer from limited generalization and degrade in cross-dataset evaluation. In this paper, we systematically investigate whether general-purpose foundation models (FMs) and multimodal large language models (MLLMs) encode PAD-relevant and MAD-relevant information, and how such models can best be deployed for both tasks. We study five approaches with increasing access to the internal information of the model: (i) zero-shot prompting of off-the-shelf MLLMs; (ii) training a shallow model on the next-token logit probabilities at the output of the MLLM; (iii) parameter-efficient fine-tuning on task-specific question-answer data, yielding two specialized MLLMs, called PADLLM and MADLLM, which additionally provide textual reasoning for their decisions; (iv) linear probing of frozen vision encoders; and (v) fine-tuning of vision encoders of FMs and MLLMs. We benchmark 16 open-weight MLLMs and 30 vision encoder backbones on four PAD datasets (MSU-MFSD, CASIA-FASD, Replay-Attack, and OULU-NPU) and four MAD datasets (FFHQ, FRGC, FRLL, and FERET). Our experiments show that FMs and MLLMs can achieve significant performance for PAD and MAD. In addition, the fine-tuned models achieve state-of-the-art detection performance in cross-dataset evaluation, indicating that general-purpose pretrained representations carry substantial attack-relevant information. Source code of all our experiments will be publicly released.

</details>


### [136] [Beyond Global Realism: Virtual Try-On Evaluation and Optimization with Dimension-wise Garment Fidelity Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29804)
*Kaidong Zhang,Yukang Ding,Xiaoyu Liu,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DAT框架，将虚拟试穿的服装一致性解耦为七个可解释维度（轮廓、颜色、领口袖型、主要装饰结构、材质纹理、细节保真度、logo保留），每个维度建模为离散属性预测任务。通过50K弱监督+10K多模型投票精标注的两阶段训练，并结合加权交叉熵损失缓解类别不平衡。该评估模型还可作为强化学习的奖励信号优化Qwen-Image-Edit的VTON生成。8B参数模型在balanced accuracy、SROCC、PLCC上超越Gemini-3.1、GPT-5.5等专有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VTON评估指标（PSNR、SSIM、KID、FID）无法有效衡量生成服装与参考服装之间的多维度一致性，缺乏对服装特征保真度的细粒度、可解释的评估手段。

Method: 1) 将服装一致性分解为七个可解释评估维度，每个维度构建为属性级离散预测任务；2) 两阶段训练：50K大规模弱监督预训练+10K高质量多模型投票标注精修；3) 加权交叉熵损失处理标签不平衡；4) 评估模型输出的维度奖励自适应聚合，集成到Qwen-Image-Edit的强化学习优化中。

Result: 8B参数的DAT模型在balanced accuracy、SROCC和PLCC上达到SOTA，超越Gemini-3.1、Qwen3.7-plus、GPT-5.5等专有大模型；同时作为奖励信号有效提升VTON生成质量。

Conclusion: 多维度解耦的评估范式比整体式指标更能捕捉服装保真度的细粒度差异，且评估模型可作为可微分奖励信号反哺生成模型的强化学习优化，形成评估-生成闭环。

Abstract: Virtual try-on (VTON) requires not only realistic generation but also faithful preservation of garment characteristics. However, existing evaluation metrics such as PSNR, SSIM, KID and FID struggle to measure the consistency between the generated and reference garments, particularly in capturing the multi-dimensional characteristics of garment fidelity. To address this, we propose DAT: a Dimension-wise Assessment framework for virtual Try-on, which decomposes garment consistency into seven interpretable dimensions: silhouette, color, neckline and sleeve shape, major decoration and structure, material texture, fine-detail fidelity, and logo preservation, each formulated as a discrete attribute-level prediction task. To train this specialized assessment model, we adopt a two-stage learning paradigm comprising large-scale weak supervision on 50K samples, followed by refinement on 10K higher-quality annotations obtained via multi-model voting. Furthermore, we employ weighted cross-entropy loss to mitigate the severe label imbalance inherent across evaluation dimensions. Beyond its role as an evaluation framework, the assessment model can be integrated into reinforcement learning optimization of Qwen-Image-Edit for VTON, where dimension-wise rewards are adaptively aggregated to emphasize under-optimized aspects during training. Experimental results show that our method (8B parameters) achieves state-of-the-art performance in terms of balanced accuracy, SROCC, and PLCC, outperforming strong proprietary models such as Gemini-3.1, Qwen3.7-plus, and GPT-5.5, while also serving as an effective optimization signal for reward-guided VTON generation

</details>
