Fixed-checkpoint tests further establish reliance on terrain and WorldCover without establishing a clean-input performance benefit, while target semantics and the later WorldCover prior restrict the evaluation to retrospective all-water segmentation. These results show that aggregate metrics remain useful for ranking complete configurations, but ranking stability, component attribution, input reliance, and deployment scope require distinct evidence. Performance evaluation should therefore match the evidence reported to the claim being made.

</details>


### [239] [Rad-R: A Raw-ADC Radar Dataset and Capture-Invariant SSM for Hardware-Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30896)
*Mainak Mallick,Junghwan Yim,Seung-Kyum Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍Rad-R，一个基于4芯片77GHz TI MMWCAS-RF-EVM级联雷达（192虚拟通道）的原始ADC数据集，用于毫米波雷达硬件故障检测。与现有数据集不同，Rad-R为每次记录配对受控硬件故障及标定严重程度、独立物理测量、帧同步IMU/温度/GPS/相机数据。文章提出原始IQ Mamba SSM模型（RadrNet），在多个协议下评测七种视觉骨干，发现交叉严重程度泛化仍困难（最佳RadrNet-DS-CI为0.663 macro-F1，超越最强RD-CNN的0.628），并发现雷达微多普勒与IMU振动能量共变（Spearman ρ=0.41）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车毫米波雷达在感知前会因振动、天线错位、雷达罩遮挡和接收通道退化等硬件故障损坏信号，但此类故障数据稀缺，因为每种条件都需要在物理硬件上诱发并测量。现有原始雷达数据集缺乏受控故障标定和物理测量，无法系统研究硬件故障对雷达感知的影响。

Method: 构建Rad-R原始ADC数据集（单会话采集），每段记录配对被控硬件故障（标定严重程度）、独立物理严重度测量以及帧同步的IMU/温度/GPS/相机流。提出RadrNet，基于原始IQ数据的Mamba状态空间模型，含变体RadrNet-DS（绝对相位）和RadrNet-DS-CI（用每帧标准化幅度与相对chirp间相位替换绝对相位）。在within-clip、chirp-wise anytime、few-shot cross-capture和受控交叉严重程度协议下评测七种视觉骨干。

Result: Within-clip表现接近饱和（>0.98 macro-F1），但交叉严重程度泛化困难（RadrNet-DS降至0.49）。RadrNet-DS-CI在受控基准中排名第一（0.663 vs 0.628，三种子），并在anytime和few-shot协议中领先。交叉模态分析显示雷达微多普勒与IMU振动能量共变（ρ=0.41）。数据集和代码将以宽松许可公开。

Conclusion: 毫米波雷达硬件故障检测的交叉严重程度泛化仍是开放难题；使用每帧标准化幅度和相对相位而非绝对相位能显著提升泛化。Rad-R数据集为社区提供首个带受控物理标定和丰富传感同步的故障级原始ADC数据资源。

Abstract: Automotive mmWave radar can develop vibration, antenna misalignment, radome blockage, and receive-channel degradation that corrupt the signal before perception begins. Data for these faults are scarce because each condition must be induced and measured on physical hardware. We introduce Rad-R, a raw-ADC dataset captured with a 4-chip 77GHz TI MMWCAS-RF-EVM cascade (192 virtual channels). Unlike existing raw-radar datasets, Rad-R pairs each recording with a controlled hardware fault at a calibrated severity, an independent physical severity measurement, and frame-synchronised IMU, temperature, GPS, and camera streams. Rad-R is a single-session dataset, so our generalisation claims are confined to a controlled cross-severity protocol in which train and test use physically distinct captures. A reproducible benchmark evaluates seven representative vision backbones and the proposed raw-IQ Mamba SSM (RadrNet) under within-clip, chirp-wise anytime, few-shot cross-capture, and controlled cross-severity protocols. Within-clip performance is near-saturated ($>0.98$ macro-F1), whereas cross-severity generalisation remains difficult: the absolute-phase RadrNet-DS falls to $0.49$ macro-F1. RadrNet-DS-CI replaces absolute phase with per-frame-standardised magnitude and relative chirp-to-chirp phase and ranks first on the controlled benchmark ($0.663$ vs. $0.628$ for the strongest RD-CNN; three seeds); the RadrNet family also leads on the anytime and few-shot budgets. A descriptive cross-modal analysis further finds that radar micro-Doppler covaries with independently measured IMU vibration energy (pooled Spearman $ρ=0.41$ across conditions). The complete dataset and code will be released publicly under permissive licences.

</details>


### [240] [Uncertainty-Aware Trajectory Forecasting from Imperfect Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30899)
*Stephane Da Silva Martins,Victor Petrovic,Emanuel Aldea,Sylvie Le Hégarat-Mascle*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将多目标跟踪器产生的不确定性信号作为信息传播给轨迹预测模型。每个观测状态被编码为高斯分布的不确定状态表示，协方差通过全方差定律结合检测级定位不确定性和关联级歧义。现有预测骨干只需最小架构改动即可适配：输入为高斯观测，输出为高斯预测。训练时结合时间相关的Ornstein-Uhlenbeck扰动和知识蒸馏（教师网络在干净轨迹上训练）。在Oxford Town Centre、VIRAT（真实跟踪器输出）及ETH/UCY（伪检测协议）上验证，提升位移精度和概率预测的可靠性-锐度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实部署中轨迹预测依赖不完美的多目标跟踪器产生的轨迹，包含定位抖动、检测不稳定和数据关联歧义。现有预测模型在干净历史轨迹上训练和评估，忽略了这些结构化噪声。作者认为跟踪派生的可靠性线索应作为信息信号传播给预测器，而非忽略或去噪。

Method: 提出即插即用的不确定性感知框架：1) 每个观测状态建模为高斯分布，协方差通过全方差定律融合检测级定位不确定性与关联级歧义；2) 输入轨迹用高斯观测表示，预测轨迹输出高斯预测而非确定性坐标；3) 训练时使用时间相关的Ornstein-Uhlenbeck扰动模拟结构化观测噪声，并结合响应级知识蒸馏，使预测器在噪声观测下保持鲁棒。

Result: 在Oxford Town Centre和VIRAT数据集上使用真实跟踪器输出，配合ETH/UCY伪检测协议，实验表明该方法在位移精度和概率预测的可靠性-锐度权衡上均优于基线。

Conclusion: 跟踪不确定性（检测定位不确定性和关联歧义）可以作为有价值的信号传播到轨迹预测器中，通过高斯状态表示和噪声鲁棒训练，提升预测精度与概率预测的校准质量。该方法对现有预测骨干可实现最小改动集成。

Abstract: Most trajectory forecasting models are trained on clean annotated histories, and are often evaluated under the same idealized assumption, although practical deployments rely on trajectories produced by imperfect multi-object trackers. The real-world observations exhibit localization jitter, missed or unstable detections, and data-association ambiguity, which are usually either ignored or removed through denoising. This paper instead treats tracking-derived reliability cues as an informative signal to be propagated to the predictor. We propose a plug-in uncertainty-aware formulation in which each observed state is encoded as an uncertain state representation, modeled by a Gaussian distribution whose covariance combines detection-level localization uncertainty and association-level ambiguity through the law of total variance. Existing backbones are adapted with minimal architectural changes: input trajectories are represented as Gaussian observations, and predicted trajectories are produced as Gaussian forecasts rather than deterministic coordinates. To train predictors that remain robust under structured observation noise, we combine temporally correlated Ornstein-Uhlenbeck perturbations with response-based knowledge distillation from a teacher trained on clean trajectories. Experiments on Oxford Town Centre and VIRAT using real tracker outputs, together with a complementary ETH/UCY pseudo-detection protocol, show that the proposed formulation improves displacement accuracy and the reliability-sharpness trade-off of probabilistic forecasts.

</details>


### [241] [Audio-Driven Adversarial Defense for 3D Talking Face Generation with totally Visual Fidelity Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30951)
*Rui-Qing Sun,Chen-Hao Cui,Hui-Yang Zhao,Tian Lan,Zhijing Wu,Xian-Ling Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对音频驱动3D说话人脸生成的不可感知音频防御方法，利用心理声学掩蔽将保护性扰动隐藏在语音信号的人耳不可感知频段中，在减少视觉畸变的同时有效抑制人脸动画生成，实现隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的主动防御主要作用于视觉域，通过在人脸区域注入扰动干扰身份获取，但由于人脸具有强结构先验和社会敏感性，这类扰动往往损害视觉质量，且容易被缩放等常见真实世界变换削弱。

Method: 将防御从视觉模态转移到音频模态，利用心理声学掩蔽原理，在语音信号中感知掩蔽的频率区域内隐藏保护性扰动，在最小化听觉感知失真的同时破坏音频驱动的人脸动画生成。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法能有效降低3D说话人脸生成质量，同时保持良好的感知质量。

Conclusion: 心理声学引导的音频扰动是面向隐私保护的肖像防护的一个实用且有前景的方向。

Abstract: The rapid development of generative portrait models has raised growing concerns about privacy leakage and identity misuse. In particular, audio-driven 3D talking face generation can reconstruct a reusable 3D portrait of a target person from a monocular video and animate it with arbitrary speech, making realistic identity impersonation alarmingly practical. Existing proactive defenses mainly operate in the visual domain by injecting subtle perturbations into acial regions to disrupt identity acquisition. However, such perturbations often compromise visual quality due to the strong structural priors and social sensitivity of human faces, and are easily weakened by common real-world transformations such as resizing. To overcome these limitations, we propose an imperceptible audio defense for audio-driven 3D talking face generation by shifting protection from the visual modality to the audio modality. Specifically,we exploit psychoacoustic masking to hide protective perturbations within perceptually masked frequency regions of the speech signal, thereby reducing perceptual distortion while suppressing reliable facial animation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed method effectively degrades 3D talking face generation while preserving favorable perceptual quality. These findings highlight psychoacoustically guided audio perturbations as a practical and promising direction for privacy-preserving portrait protection.

</details>


### [242] [LOCI: A Locator-Critic with Refinement Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30959)
*Walid Bousselham,Mathilde Caron,Arsha Nagrani,Cordelia Schmid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LOCI（Locator-Critic），一个无需训练的框架，将视觉搜索与证据验证解耦。Locator智能体提出候选视觉证据，Critic智能体评估其相关性和充分性，通过迭代精化循环逐步改善证据，直到足以回答问题。在多个复杂视觉基准上达到SOTA，显著提升开源和闭源VLM准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为VLM在复杂视觉理解任务上失败的核心问题不是高层推理能力不足，而是无法定位图像中的关键细节。VLM常常基于错误的感知基础生成看似合理但错误的推理，因此需要一种机制来提升视觉证据定位能力。

Method: LOCI采用训练免费的两智能体框架：Locator负责提出候选视觉证据，Critic负责评估证据的相关性与充分性。两个智能体通过迭代精化循环协同工作，逐步改进证据质量直到足以回答给定问题，实现视觉搜索与证据验证的解耦。

Result: 在多个复杂视觉基准上取得SOTA：开源模型Qwen3-VL提升V* +12.1、HR-Bench +5.8、VisualProbe-Hard +11.2；闭源模型Gemini 2.5 Pro提升V* +8.9、HR-Bench +4.3、VisualProbe-Hard +4.8。

Conclusion: 解耦式自校正的证据定位-验证过程能有效解决VLM视觉感知失败问题，且无需训练即可适用于多种VLM，带来显著的准确率提升。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) still struggle on tasks requiring complex visual understanding. We argue that the core issue is not high-level reasoning, but instead failing to locate critical details in the image. Due to this shortcoming, VLMs generate often plausible but incorrect reasoning based on flawed perceptual grounding. To address this, we propose Locator-Critic (LOCI), a training-free framework that decouples visual search from evidence verification. LOCI employs a Locator agent to propose candidate visual evidence and a separate Critic agent to evaluate its relevance and sufficiency. These agents engage in an iterative refinement loop, progressively improving the evidence until it is adequate to answer the given question. This decoupled, self-correcting process yields substantial performance gains, achieving state-of-the-art results on multiple complex visual benchmarks. LOCI improves accuracy for both open-weight models like Qwen3-VL (+12.1 on V*, +5.8 on HR-Bench and +11.2 on VisualProbe-Hard) and proprietary models like Gemini 2.5 Pro (+8.9 on V*, +4.3 on HR-Bench, +4.8 on VisualProbe-Hard).

</details>


### [243] [Vision Models Predict Urban Scene Appraisal with Limited Neural Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30964)
*Kaizhen Tan,Yuantao Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文检验预训练视觉嵌入是否能像人类一样组织城市场景的表征，而非仅仅预测人类评分。利用63名成年人观看56个柏林街景的公开EEG数据，作者发现17种特征空间（含DINOv2、语言监督、自监督等）与人脑神经几何对应度普遍很低（11.0%-29.6%）——最佳模型DINOv2 ViT-B仅达噪声下限的29.6%，甚至Gabor能量描述符与最佳模型无差别且优于所有语言监督模型。但同一嵌入预测人类评分可高达r=0.87。这表明高分预测能力不能证明模型以人脑方式表征场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉嵌入用于建模人类对城市场景的评价，但其验证几乎完全依赖预测人类评分的准确率。作者指出，高预测准确率并不等同于嵌入以人类感知方式组织场景——需要将"预测行为"与"表征几何是否符合大脑"这两个性质分开检验。

Method: 使用公开EEG数据（63名成年人，56个柏林街景），估计场景随时间变化的表征几何；计算该几何中可解释的比例；测试其与17种特征空间的对应性，覆盖语言监督、自监督、类别监督、密集预测训练，两个数量级的模型规模，并包含可解释控制（如Gabor能量）。同时对比嵌入预测人类评分的能力（r=0.87），并测试特征重加权对两类指标的影响。

Result: 对应度整体很低：最佳DINOv2 ViT-B为29.6%噪声下限，整体范围11.0%-29.6%；Gabor能量描述符与最佳模型无显著差异且优于所有语言监督模型。模型内深层仍匹配更晚的神经响应（层次对应保留），但同一嵌入预测评分高达r=0.87，两类指标不随模型共同变化；向神经几何重加权特征会降低每个模型的评分预测能力。

Conclusion: 预测街道被如何评价只是模型以人脑方式表示街道的弱证据。该基准使用公开数据且无需训练，评估新表示仅需55张图像的嵌入。

Abstract: Pretrained vision embeddings are increasingly used as general-purpose representations for modelling how people appraise urban scenes, and are validated almost entirely by how well they predict human ratings. High predictive accuracy does not establish that these embeddings organise scenes as human perception does. We test the two properties separately against brain data. Using openly released EEG from 63 adults who viewed and rated 56 Berlin street scenes, we estimate the representational geometry of the scenes over time, the proportion of that geometry that is explainable at all, and its correspondence with seventeen feature spaces spanning language-supervised, self-supervised, category-supervised and dense-prediction training, two orders of magnitude of scale, and interpretable controls. Correspondence is low throughout: the best representation, DINOv2 ViT-B, reaches 29.6% of the lower bound of the noise ceiling, the panel spans 11.0% to 29.6%, and a Gabor energy descriptor is indistinguishable from the best model while outperforming every language-supervised model tested. Within a model, deeper layers still match later neural responses, so the hierarchical correspondence found for object recognition survives even at this low overall level. The same embeddings predict held-out appraisal ratings well, up to r = 0.87, and the two measures do not track each other across models; reweighting features towards the neural geometry lowers appraisal prediction for every model tested, against a control of matched dimensionality. Predicting how a street is appraised is therefore weak evidence that a model represents the street as the brain does. The benchmark uses only public data and requires no training, so evaluating a new representation needs only its embeddings for 55 images.

</details>


### [244] [MR-JEPA: A General Purpose Video Foundation Model for Cardiac MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30975)
*Athira J. Jacob,Puneet Sharma,Dorin Comaniciu,Daniel Rueckert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MR-JEPA是一个针对心脏磁共振成像(CMR)的自监督视频基础模型，通过tubelet tokenization和时空掩码增强，在10,505例患者的多种序列数据(cine/LGE/mapping)上无标注预训练，在6个下游任务(射血分数、心肌应变、疾病检测)上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMR产生丰富的时序视频和空间堆叠数据，但现有深度学习方法大多处理单个2D切片，丢弃了重要的时空上下文信息。此外，已有的CMR视频模型局限于cine单一序列，缺乏对多序列数据的统一建模能力。

Method: 将LeJEPA扩展为3D时空输入，采用tubelet tokenization对视频片段进行分块，结合时空掩码增强进行自监督预训练，并从2D CMR基础模型进行初始化。在cine、LGE、mapping多序列数据(10,505患者、两中心)上无标注预训练，下游采用统一的多视角门控注意力架构对冻结编码器进行评估。

Result: 在5个回归任务上全部优于对比方法(LV EF MAE 4.79%, r=0.764; GLS MAE 1.87, r=0.805)，应变任务相比基线降低21-27%的MAE；疾病检测macro AUC达到0.868，与特定领域基线相当。

Conclusion: MR-JEPA展示了统一视频编码器在多样化CMR序列上鲁棒的多视角利用潜力，证明了自监督视频预训练在临床心脏定量分析和诊断中的有效性。

Abstract: Cardiac magnetic resonance imaging (CMR) produces rich sequential data such as temporal cine videos and spatial LGE/mapping stacks, yet most deep learning approaches process individual 2D slices, discarding this context. We present MR-JEPA, a self-supervised video foundation model for CMR that extends LeJEPA to 3D spatiotemporal inputs through tubelet tokenization, spatiotemporal masking augmentation, and initialization from a 2D CMR foundation model. Unlike prior CMR video models limited to cine data, MR-JEPA is pretrained on multi-sequence data (cine, LGE, mapping) from 10,505 patients across two centers without annotations. We evaluate the frozen encoder on six downstream tasks using a unified multi-view gated attention architecture: LV ejection fraction, RV ejection fraction, three myocardial strains (GLS, GCS, GRS), and four-class disease detection. MR-JEPA outperforms other compared methods on all five regression tasks, including both a domain-specific CMR model pretrained on more data with text supervision and a natural-video foundation model, achieving an LV EF MAE of 4.79% (r =0.764) and a GLS MAE of 1.87 (r=0.805), with 21-27% MAE reductions over baselines on strain tasks. For disease detection, MR-JEPA achieved a macro AUG of 0.868, remaining competitive with the domain-specific baseline despite using a fully self-supervised pretraining objective. These results demonstrate the potential of a unified video encoder for robust, multi-view utilization of diverse CMR sequences in clinical cardiac quantification and diagnosis.

</details>


### [245] [Multi-View Reflective Surface Inspection via Semantic-Saliency Cross-Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30997)
*Van-Giang Nguyen,Thanh-Tuan Tran,Xuan-Hieu Phan,Xiem HoangVan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多视角反射表面（智能手机盖板玻璃）缺陷检测框架。每个RGB视角由共享的per-view expert处理：VLM生成类别感知的语义框，normal-reference重建分支提供类别无关的显著性图。语义框与显著性的空间一致性作为支持证据用于重新排序语义建议，无需修改坐标或将显著性作为真值。产品层面的证据记录无需跨视角配准即可合并。在282张产线图像上，语义-显著性关联将AP50从52.6%提升至62.6%；在94个产品上，三视角交叉证据召回率R_prod@0.5从最佳单视角的75.5%提升至88.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反射性智能手机盖板玻璃的缺陷检测存在两大挑战：缺陷在特定视角下弱可见性，以及视觉证据在空间上的歧义性。单一固定视角无法可靠检测此类缺陷，需要多视角信息和语义-显著性互补证据来提升检测可靠性。

Method: 提出多视角检测框架：(1) 每个视角由一个共享的per-view expert处理；(2) VLM生成类别感知的语义边界框建议；(3) normal-reference重建分支生成类别无关的显著性图；(4) 利用语义框与显著性图的空间一致性作为重排证据（re-ranking），不修改语义框坐标，也不将显著性视为真值；(5) 产品级别聚合证据记录，无需跨视角配准。

Result: 在282张产线图像上，语义-显著性关联重排将AP50从52.6%提升至62.6%（+10个百分点）。在94个产品上，三视角交叉证据的产品级召回率R_prod@0.5从最佳单视角的75.5%提升至88.3%（+12.8个百分点）。实验结果支持语义-显著性交叉验证与额外光学观测在反射表面检测中的互补作用。

Conclusion: 语义-显著性交叉验证和多视角观测在反射表面缺陷检测中是互补的：语义信息提供类别感知的粗定位，显著性提供类别无关的细粒度证据，两者结合可显著提升检测性能。方法避免了复杂的三维配准，仅通过产品级证据融合即可实现跨视角增益。

Abstract: Reflective smartphone cover glass is challenging to inspect from a single fixed viewpoint because defect visibility varies with viewing geometry and specular reflections. This gives rise to two practical challenges: defects may be weakly observable from certain viewpoints, while the available visual evidence may remain spatially ambiguous. To address these issues, we propose a multi-view inspection framework in which each RGB observation is processed by a shared per-view expert. A vision-language model (VLM) produces class-aware semantic boxes, while a normal-reference reconstruction branch provides class-agnostic saliency. Their spatial agreement is used as supporting evidence to rank semantic proposals without modifying their coordinates or treating saliency as ground truth. The resulting evidence records are combined at product level without cross-view registration. On 282 production-line images, semantic-saliency association improves $AP_{50}$ from 52.6% to 62.6% by re-ranking fixed semantic proposals. Across 94 products, cross-view evidence recall $R_{\rm prod}@0.5$ increases from 75.5% for the best single view to 88.3% using all three views. These results support the complementary roles of semantic-saliency cross-verification and additional optical observations in reflective-surface inspection.

</details>


### [246] [From Intent to Evidence: Policy-Steered Multi-Strategy Retrieval for Long-Video Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31005)
*Can Zhang,Baofeng Zhang,Xiaotian Han,Junyuan Shang,Yuchen Ding,Shuohuan Wang,Dianhai Yu,Ruirui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VESTA是一种无需训练的长视频智能体，通过路由条件化的"采集-验证-整合"循环，根据证据类型（聚焦、回忆或对比检索）自动推断证据采集策略和记账策略，在视频问答任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频智能体统一采用单一证据采集行为，未区分证据是集中分布、需要广泛覆盖还是需要区分竞争假设，导致在实质性推理开始前就失败。为每个问题指定细粒度解决方案又限制了自主探索。

Method: 提出VESTA，无需训练的agent，采用路由条件化的采集-验证-整合循环。意图路由器推断证据采集策略（聚焦、回忆或对比检索于共享的视觉-语音场景索引）及证据记账策略。策略驱动的检索产生参考，多模态证据操作转换为观察；Reasoner可自由验证、用中间发现重新查询或检查检索集外区域。时间证据分类账将观察整合为自适应的压缩视图（时间位置、来源、覆盖、冲突、验证结果和假设支持），暴露缺失和未解决证据以指导后续采集；最终答案优先验证过的观察。

Result: 在Video-MME-v2上，VESTA相比VideoARM平均准确率提升2.7个点，并在全部六个报告指标上均有提升。在LongVideoBench、EgoSchema和LVBench上（共享查询时模型），LongVideoBench长子集提升6.9个点，LVBench提升1.5个点，在EgoSchema上与VideoARM持平。

Conclusion: VESTA表明通过自适应证据采集策略而非统一行为，可以显著提升长视频智能体的推理能力，且无需训练即可实现。

Abstract: Existing long-video agents acquire evidence through one uniform behavior, ignoring whether the required evidence is concentrated, requires broad occurrence coverage, or must discriminate competing hypotheses---which can cause failure before substantive reasoning begins. Prescribing a fine-grained solution procedure for every question is not a satisfactory remedy, as it restricts autonomous exploration. We propose VESTA, a training-free long-video agent organized as a route-conditioned acquire--verify--consolidate loop. Before exploration, an intent router infers an evidence-acquisition policy---focused, recall, or contrastive retrieval over a shared visual--speech scene index---together with an evidence-accounting policy that configures the evidence view maintained during exploration. Policy-steered retrieval yields provisional references that multimodal evidence operations convert into observations, while the Reasoner remains free to verify them, re-query using intermediate findings, or inspect regions outside the retrieved set. A temporal evidence ledger consolidates observations into an adaptive, compressed view of temporal location, provenance, coverage, conflicts, verification outcomes, and hypothesis support, exposing missing and unresolved evidence to guide subsequent acquisition; finalization prioritizes verified observations. On Video-MME-v2, VESTA improves average accuracy by 2.7 points over VideoARM and gains across all six reported metrics. On LongVideoBench, EgoSchema, and LVBench under shared query-time models, it improves by 6.9 points on the LongVideoBench long subset and 1.5 on LVBench, and matches VideoARM on EgoSchema.

</details>


### [247] [SMG: Semantic Motion Graph for Monocular Dynamic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31023)
*Haozheng Yu,Xinyu Yang,Rundong Luo,Jennifer J. Sun,Bharath Hariharan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对单目视频的动态高斯溅射过拟合与遮挡问题，提出语义运动图（SMG）方法，将高斯运动建模为低秩语义运动，利用语义一致性先验约束弱约束区域，并通过可靠图节点引导不可靠节点运动，在新增ego-exo多视角数据集及多个挑战性基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目动态高斯溅射在训练视角上易过拟合，且在遮挡或复杂场景运动下缺乏可靠正则化信号，导致弱约束区域运动估计不可靠。

Method: 构建语义运动图（SMG），将高斯运动表示为低秩语义运动；高斯运动由SMG节点驱动；利用语义与空间邻近性先验，让可靠图节点引导附近不可靠节点的运动以抑制不确定性。

Result: 在新增的ego-exo多视角数据集及多个挑战性真实世界基准上取得SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 语义一致性先验能有效约束动态场景中弱约束区域的运动估计，SMG在单目动态高斯溅射上显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: We study dynamic Gaussian Splatting from monocular videos. While recent advancements in dynamic Gaussian splatting offer a promising foundation for modeling dynamic scenes, they often overfit to the training views and fail under occlusion or complex scene motion due to the lack of reliable regularization signals in under-constrained regions. We propose Semantic Motion Graph (SMG), a novel approach models the Gaussian motion as the low-rank semantic motion. Our key insight is that the real-world scene motion is often structured by semantic coherence: regions that are spatially close and semantically related tend to exhibit consistent dynamics. To leverage this prior, we construct SMG to model structured motion of the scene. The Gaussian motion is driven by the motion of SMG nodes. We further observe that the uncertainty of Gaussian motion arises from both unreliable off-the-shelf priors and weakly constrained regions during optimization. SMG addresses this by using reliable graph nodes to guide the motion of nearby unreliable nodes. To evaluate dynamic Gaussian splatting under challenging real-world scenarios, we introduce a new multiview dataset collected under an ego-exo setup. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SMG achieves state-of-the-art performance on monocular dynamic Gaussian splatting across challenging real-world benchmarks. Project page: https://smg-gaussian.github.io/.

</details>


### [248] [Analytic Dynamics: Learning Physics-Grounded Representation for Fast Intrinsic Dynamics Inference from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31025)
*Jailing Lin,Jikuan Zhang,Jianhua Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Analytic Dynamics，一种前馈（feed-forward）动力学推理框架，在视觉观测与内在动力学之间引入物理学基础的中间动力学表示（位置、位移、形变梯度场等特权物理状态），通过将视觉模型与这一物理有偏表示对齐，实现从单目视频中进行高效、准确且可泛化的材料模型分类与参数回归，并配套发布了动力学数据生成管线和基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉观测与内在动力学之间存在根本性鸿沟。现有方法要么依赖逐场景优化的昂贵手段（效率与可扩展性差），要么直接从视觉证据映射到内在动力学而缺乏中间物理抽象，容易受外观和几何捷径影响，推理不鲁棒。

Method: 提出一种包含中间物理学grounded动力学表示的前馈推理框架。利用仿真中可获得的特权物理状态（位置、位移、形变梯度场）学习结构化动力学表示，该表示难以仅从视觉观测中直接发现。通过将视觉表示与此空间对齐，为视觉模型注入物理学有偏归纳偏置，用于材料模型分类和参数回归。同时构建了包含成对的物理状态轨迹、渲染视频和真实材料模型/参数的数据生成管线与基准。

Result: 大量实验表明，Analytic Dynamics能够从单目视频中实现高效、准确且可泛化的动力学推理，相较于依赖逐场景优化或缺乏物理中间抽象的现有方法有明显优势。

Conclusion: 通过在视觉观测与内在动力学之间引入可学习的物理学有偏表示，可以弥合视觉证据与动力学参数之间的鸿沟，为前馈、高效、可泛化的物质模型推断提供了新范式。

Abstract: Inferring object dynamics from visual observations is essential for intelligent agents to reason about and interact with the physical world, yet remains challenging due to the fundamental gap between visual evidence and intrinsic dynamics. Existing methods either rely on costly per-scene optimization, limiting efficiency and scalability, or directly map visual evidence to intrinsic dynamics without intermediate physical abstractions, making them prone to appearance and geometry shortcuts. To bridge this gap, we propose Analytic Dynamics, a feed-forward dynamics inference framework that introduces an intermediate physics-grounded dynamics representation between visual observations and intrinsic dynamics. Specifically, we leverage privileged physical states, including position, displacement, and deformation gradient fields, which are available in simulation, to learn a structured dynamics representation that is difficult to discover from visual observations alone. By aligning visual representations with this space, we equip visual models with a physics-grounded inductive bias, guiding them to capture dynamics-relevant patterns for material model classification and parameter regression. To facilitate this research, we develop a dynamics data generation pipeline and benchmark containing paired physical state trajectories, rendered videos, and ground-truth material models and parameters. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Analytic Dynamics achieves efficient, accurate, and generalizable dynamics inference from monocular videos.

</details>


### [249] [FaceSnap: Real-Time Personalized Lightstage Facial Performance Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31033)
*Rukhshanda Hussain,Noé Artru,Emeline Got,Luiz Gustavo Hafemann,Alexandre Messier,Brandon Dearlove,Rafael M. O. Cruz,Abdallah Dib,Eric Granger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FaceSnap是一个端到端框架，通过两阶段方法将光场舞台（lightstage）面部捕捉从多视角、高计算量的流程简化为单目实时捕捉：一次多视角优化构建个性化模型（编码几何和表情依赖外观），之后仅需单个单目光场相机即可实现83fps的高保真实时面部表演捕捉，同时生成4K动态纹理。此外论文提出了Multi4D基准用于光场环境下4D面部重建的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光场舞台面部捕捉能产生生产级质量的数字人类，但多相机设置、数小时计算和大规模数据存储造成了严重的资源瓶颈，阻碍了迭代工作流。需要一个更高效的方法来在保持质量的同时实现实时捕捉。

Method: 两阶段方法：(1) 一次性多视角优化，从range-of-motion序列构建个性化模型，同时编码几何和表情依赖的外观；(2) 利用该模型，仅从单个单目光场相机进行高保真实时面部表演捕捉。提出个性化残差上采样器（personalized residual upscaler）恢复主体特定的高频细节（通用上采样器无法捕捉），联合估计几何和动态4K纹理，以83fps运行。

Result: FaceSnap达到的几何精度与完整的逐帧多视角优化相当，同时优于在生质量3D数据上训练的feed-forward方法，且仅需单相机视角。此外贡献了Multi4D公开基准，支持跨方法的拓扑不变几何比较。

Conclusion: 证明了通过一次性的个性化模型构建，可以大幅削减光场舞台捕捉的资源开销，实现单目实时高保真面部捕捉，在几何精度上不逊于全多视角优化方法，并推动了4D面部重建的标准化评估。

Abstract: Lightstage facial capture produces production-quality digital humans, but it is resource and labor-intensive. Multi-camera setups, hours of computation, and massive data storage create bottlenecks that hinder iterative workflows. This paper introduces FaceSnap, an end-to-end framework that streamlines capture via a two-stage approach. First, a one-time multi-view optimization from a range-of-motion sequence builds a personalized model encoding both geometry and expression-dependent appearance. This model then enables high-fidelity real-time facial performance capture from a single monocular lightstage camera, with no further multi-view capture required. FaceSnap jointly estimates geometry and dynamic 4K texture at 83 fps. The 4K texture is produced by a novel personalized residual upscaler that recovers subject-specific high-frequency detail, which generic upscalers fail to capture. FaceSnap achieves geometric accuracy competitive with full per-frame multi-view optimization while outperforming feed-forward methods trained on production-quality 3D data, all from a single camera view. Finally, we introduce Multi4D, a public benchmark for evaluating 4D facial reconstruction methods in lightstage environments, enabling topology-invariant geometric comparison across methods.

</details>


### [250] [Segmentation of Bovid Dentition Under Imperfect Annotations: A Comparative Study of Convolutional and Attention Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31052)
*Keith G. Mills,Evan B. Sanders,Gregory J. Matthews,Juliet K. Brophy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对多种语义分割架构（从卷积骨干到视觉Transformer）在B.O.V.I.D.数据集（高分辨率牛科动物牙齿照片及手工标注分割掩膜）上进行了比较研究，评估多种预处理和对齐技术以缓解标注不完美问题。研究发现预处理选择对Dice和mIoU等定量指标影响有限，但对预测掩膜的定性质量影响显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义分割DNN依赖精心设计的掩膜标签，但手工标注的掩膜往往不完美或不对齐，会干扰模型学习效果。本文旨在研究当训练标签存在缺陷时，不同分割架构和预处理方法的鲁棒性表现。

Method: 在B.O.V.I.D.数据集上系统比较多种分割架构（卷积骨干和视觉Transformer），并应用多种预处理和对齐技术来处理手工艺掩膜标签的缺陷，随后在定量指标（Dice、mIoU）和定性结果上评估各方法的效果。

Result: 预处理和对齐技术对Dice分数和mIoU等定量指标的提升有限，但对预测掩膜的定性质量有显著影响。

Conclusion: 在标签质量不完美的场景下，需要超越定量指标来评估模型性能，定性质量同样重要；预处理选择的重要性体现在定性而非纯粹定量层面。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation decomposes an image into distinct mask regions corresponding to different object categories, such as people, cars, signs or buildings. Advances in machine learning (ML) have shifted this task away from traditional rule-based heuristics such as edge detection, towards deep neural networks (DNN) that learn to classify pixels directly. However, semantic segmentation DNNs crucially depend on expertly designed mask targets to learn from, and imperfect or misaligned masks can interfere with a model's ability to learn effectively.
  This paper presents a comparative study of segmentation architectures, ranging from convolutional backbones to vision transformers, applied to the B.O.V.I.D. dataset, a corpus of high-resolution bovid dental photographs paired with hand-made segmentation masks not originally designed for ML-based training. We evaluate a range of preprocessing and alignment techniques to mitigate the resulting label imperfections. We find that while these preprocessing choices have limited effect on quantitative metrics such as Dice score and mIoU, their qualitative impact on predicted masks is substantial.

</details>


### [251] [Identity-Conditioned Latent Consistency Distillation for Face Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31053)
*Tiago Kienen Chaves,Bernardo Biesseck,David Menotti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将身份条件的人脸合成从Arc2Face扩散模型蒸馏到潜一致性模型（LCM），以极少的迭代次数实现大幅加速（4.36×），同时保持图像质量和生成性能的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型迭代采样过程计算开销大，在大规模合成人脸数据集生成（用于人脸识别训练）时成本过高。需要大量不同姿态、表情、年龄的样本，因此迫切需要降低生成成本同时保持高质量。

Method: 将Arc2Face（教师模型）从文本到图像管线适配为embedding到人脸（用ArcFace身份embedding替换文本提示），然后通过潜一致性蒸馏将知识蒸馏到学生LCM模型，实现仅需少量迭代即可完成身份条件人脸生成。

Result: 推理时间从Arc2Face的2.102秒/图降至0.4819秒/图（4.36×加速）。FID指标上，CelebA近持平（13.921 vs 12.928），WebFace42M上超越教师（9.317 vs 9.802），Synth-500有中等差距但AgeDB相当。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过任务特定的潜一致性蒸馏可以显著加速Arc2Face，同时保持大规模合成人脸生成的高质量，为高效构建合成人脸数据集提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved strong results in high-fidelity image synthesis, but their iterative sampling process makes large-scale generation computationally expensive. This limitation is especially relevant when generating synthetic face datasets for face recognition, where a large number of subjects with many samples in different poses, expressions, ages, etc., are required. In this work, we show that identity-conditioned face synthesis can be performed at a substantially lower computational cost by a latent Consistency Model with few iterations, without compromising image quality. For training, we distill knowledge from the foundation Diffusion Model Arc2Face (teacher) by adapting its original text-to-image pipeline to an embedding-to-face setting, replacing textual prompts with ArcFace identity embeddings. Our distilled model (student) generates identity-conditioned face images with an average inference time of 0.4819 seconds per image, compared with 2.102 seconds for Arc2Face, resulting in a 4.36$\times$ speed-up. Quantitative results, based on FID scores, show that the distilled model remains competitive with Arc2Face across all evaluation protocols. On 100k generated images, it achieves near-parity on CelebA (13.921 vs. 12.928) and outperforms the teacher on WebFace42M (9.317 vs. 9.802). Further evaluations on Synth-500 and AgeDB show a moderate performance gap for the former but comparable results for the latter. These results indicate that Arc2Face can be accelerated through task-specific latent consistency distillation while preserving high image quality for large-scale synthetic face generation. Our proposal is publicly available at https://github.com/UFPR-IPASP-PR/FaceRec-IdentityConsistency.

</details>


### [252] [Multimodal Shared Latent Representation of Narration, Microscope and iOCT Images for Phase Recognition in Vitreoretinal Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31065)
*Onur Izmitlioglu,Shervin Dehghani,Tarek Ghannoum,Benedikt Schworm,Nassir Navab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个以显微镜视图为共享锚点的框架，桥接手术叙述和术中OCT（iOCT）三种模态，无需完全同步的三模态数据集。通过对比对齐将合成域的结构先验迁移到缺少iOCT的真实视频，并用双头MS-TCN++联合预测宏观和微观手术阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻璃体视网膜手术的阶段识别对上下文感知的计算机辅助反馈至关重要，但同步的多模态术中数据（尤其显微镜视图和iOCT）稀缺，限制了复制外科医生自然多模态整合的方法。手术叙述在线丰富且提供语义监督，但此前缺乏对三种模态联合建模的探索。

Method: 以显微镜视图为共享锚点，利用真实显微镜-叙述视频和合成（同步显微镜+工具对齐iOCT）数据集。对比对齐将合成域的结构先验迁移到缺少iOCT的真实数据。双头MS-TCN++整合嵌入进行宏观和微观阶段联合预测。无需完全同步的三模态数据集。

Result: 在真实玻璃体视网膜手术上，宏观阶段识别从零样本基线提升（平均F1从0.38到0.53）。微观阶段在合成数据上定量验证，在真实手术中定性展示。是首个将显微镜视图、iOCT B扫描和手术叙述统一到共享潜在空间的工作。

Conclusion: 该框架证明无需完全同步的三模态数据即可实现多模态手术阶段识别，为更精细的器械-组织测量估计提供了探索性路径，开启了统一显微镜、iOCT和叙述模态的新方向。

Abstract: Surgical phase recognition is key to context-aware computer-assisted feedback in vitreoretinal procedures, yet the scarcity of synchronized multimodal intraoperative data, particularly microscope views and intraoperative OCT, limits approaches that aim to replicate the multimodal integration surgeons perform naturally. Surgical narration, by contrast, is abundantly available online and offers rich semantic supervision. Prior work has mainly explored pairwise contrastive learning (e.g., intraoperative OCT-microscope or microscope-narration), leaving the joint modeling of all three modalities largely unexplored. We introduce a framework that uses microscope views as a shared anchor to bridge surgical narrations and intraoperative OCT (iOCT) without requiring a fully synchronized tri-modal dataset, leveraging real microscope-narration videos and a synthetic dataset of synchronized microscope video and tool-aligned iOCT pairs. Contrastive alignment transfers structural priors from the synthetic domain to real videos lacking iOCT, and a dual-head MS-TCN++ integrates the resulting embeddings for joint macro- and micro-phase prediction. Evaluated on real vitreoretinal surgeries, our framework improves macro-phase recognition over a zero-shot baseline (mean F1 0.38 to 0.53) and provides an exploratory route to estimating fine-grained instrument-tissue measurements that are not directly observable in real microscope video alone; these micro-phase estimates are validated quantitatively on synthetic data and shown only qualitatively on real surgery. To our knowledge, this is the first work to unify microscope view, iOCT B-scans, and surgical narrations in a shared latent space for surgical phase recognition.

</details>


### [253] [LISynSeg: Data-Centric Label-to-Image Synthesis for Cross-Modality Whole-Heart Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31073)
*Jiacheng Wang,Ivana Isgum,Ipek Oguz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LISynSeg 提出一种数据驱动的训练策略：通过标签到图像合成生成心脏CT/MRI体积数据，并混合真实图像（保留标签中缺失的胸腔背景），用于增强nnU-Net在跨模态全心脏分割上的表现。在CARE Whole-Heart基准上，合成数据单独训练不如真实图像基线，但校准后的真实-合成混合训练在架构不变的前提下提升了跨模态分割效果，MRI提升大于CT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT和MRI的全心脏分割受到采集域偏移和心脏标注异质性影响。现有系统依赖架构设计、迁移学习和通用数据增强，作者想探究仅通过修改数据增强和训练监督策略（保持架构不变）能否改善跨模态分割性能，追求数据为中心的解决方案。

Method: 1) 标签到图像合成：从心脏标注图生成合成体积，并施加校准到训练队列的对比度和采集扰动；2) 真实-合成混合训练：将合成体积与真实图像混合，以保留标签（及合成图像）中缺失的胸腔背景上下文；3) 对心肌壁厚度做受控变化以建模标签变异性；4) 对不确定的血管端点采用部分监督。

Result: 在CARE Whole-Heart基准上：纯合成训练性能不及真实图像nnU-Net基线；校准后的真实-合成混合训练在不改变架构的前提下提升了跨模态分割性能，且MRI上的提升幅度大于CT。

Conclusion: 修改训练数据策略（而非架构或损失函数）可以有效提升异质性心脏数据上的模型开发效果，验证了数据为中心方法在跨模态医学分割中的价值。

Abstract: Whole-heart segmentation (WHS) in computed tomography (CT) and magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is affected by acquisition shifts and heterogeneous cardiac annotations. Existing WHS systems combine architectural design, transfer learning, and generic spatial or intensity augmentation. We investigate whether changes to data augmentation and training supervision can improve cross-modality WHS while the segmentation architecture is held constant. We present LISynSeg, a data-centric approach that augments real-image nnU-Net training with label-to-image synthesis. Synthetic volumes are generated from cardiac label maps using contrast and acquisition perturbations calibrated to the training cohort, then mixed with real images to retain thoracic context absent from the labels (and thus the synthesized images). We model cardiac label variation through controlled changes in myocardial wall thickness and partial supervision of uncertain vessel endpoints. On the CARE Whole-Heart benchmark, synthetic-only training performs worse than the real-image nnU-Net baseline, whereas calibrated real-synthetic training improves cross-modality segmentation without changing the architecture; the improvement is larger for MRI than for CT. The results show that modifying the training data strategy can benefit model development for heterogeneous cardiac data. Code and trained weights will be released at https://github.com/MedICL-VU/Care26_LISynSeg.

</details>


### [254] [Real-Time Video Anomaly Detection Using YOLO Pose Estimation and CLIP-Based Semantic Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31074)
*Vanodhya G. Warnasooriya,Amir Hajian,Watchara Ruangsang,Supavadee Aramvith*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级两阶段实时视频异常检测框架：第一阶段用YOLO v11n-pose单次前向检测行人并提取17个骨骼关键点，第二阶段用CLIP ViT-B/32编码裁剪的人体区域并与预定义异常行为文本描述计算余弦相似度。无需光流、独立姿态估计器和密度评分模块，在CUHK Avenue、ShanghaiTech Campus及朱拉隆功大学自定义室内数据集上实现约51 FPS吞吐量（较多特征基线快3.36倍），帧级AUROC分别为89.26%、70.26%和84.13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频异常检测通常依赖光流、独立姿态估计器或密度评分等多特征模块，计算开销大、实时性差。作者希望设计一种免去这些重模块的轻量级端到端框架，在保持检测精度的同时显著提升实时推理速度。

Method: 两阶段流水线：(1) 使用YOLO v11n-pose在单次前向中完成行人检测与17骨骼关键点提取；(2) 对每个裁剪行人区域用CLIP ViT-B/32编码视觉特征，与预定义的异常行为文本描述计算余弦相似度进行分类。整个架构移除光流、独立姿态估计与密度评分模块。

Result: 在NVIDIA Titan XP上端到端吞吐约51 FPS，较基线快3.36倍；CUHK Avenue、ShanghaiTech Campus及自定义室内数据集上帧级AUROC分别为89.26%、70.26%、84.13%。

Conclusion: 该框架验证了"轻量姿态检测+CLIP零样本文本对齐"在实时视频异常检测中的有效性与高效性，为资源受限场景下的实时异常监控提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We propose a lightweight two-stage framework for real-time video anomaly detection. The first stage employs YOLO v11n-pose to detect persons and extract seventeen skeletal keypoints in a single forward pass. The second stage encodes each cropped person region through CLIP ViT-B/32 and computes cosine similarity against predefined textual descriptions of anomalous behaviors. This architecture eliminates the need for optical flow, standalone pose estimators, and density-based scoring modules. Experiments on CUHK Avenue, ShanghaiTech Campus, and a custom indoor dataset collected at Chulalongkorn University demonstrate an end-to-end throughput of approximately 51 FPS on an NVIDIA Titan XP GPU, a 3.36x speedup over the multi-feature baseline, while maintaining frame-level AUROC values of 89.26%, 70.26%, and 84.13%, respectively.

</details>


### [255] [Robust retinal biometrics for patient identity verification and retrieval across age and imaging devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31094)
*Jose D. Vargas-Quiros,Dennis Bontempi,Jeroen Vermeulen,Bart Liefers,Sven Bergmann,Caroline C. W. Klaver*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于视网膜（color fundus images）的生物识别系统，利用ConvNeXtV2骨干网络结合ArcFace和triplet损失训练512维度量学习编码器，在Rotterdam Study（227,004张图像、21,851个患者-眼身份）上训练，并在Rotterdam、UK Biobank和AREDS三个数据集上验证。系统实现了0.9998级别的AUROC和97-99%的Recall@1身份检索性能，证明视网膜解剖结构是持久的生物识别信号，可用于保护纵向影像记录的数据完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者身份错误会损害纵向医学记录、研究数据库和下游临床决策。亟需一种自动、可靠的生物识别方法来验证和检索患者身份，尤其是在多设备、长随访周期的眼科影像场景中，以确保大规模纵向影像数据的完整性和可信度。

Method: 训练512维度量学习编码器，使用ConvNeXtV2骨干，联合ArcFace和triplet损失。训练数据来自Rotterdam Study的227,004张彩色眼底图像（21,851个患者-眼身份），覆盖多种成像设备和最长32.6年的随访。先用于筛查身份不一致并人工裁定，再进行验证（剔除近似重复图像后）和身份检索评估（仅用先前获取的图像），同时在UK Biobank和AREDS上做外部验证。

Result: 筛查发现0.588%、0.259%、0.164%的图像存在身份分配错误。验证模式下AUROC分别为0.9998、0.9997、0.9998。检索任务中Recall@1为99.7%、97.2%、97.6%（图库平均包含4436-8510个身份），Top-5包含正确身份的比率≥98.6%。性能在跨设备和长随访间隔下保持稳健，低图像质量和不一致的视网膜视野是主要失败原因。

Conclusion: 视网膜解剖结构是持久的生物识别信号，可用于保护纵向影像记录的完整性。该系统的卓越验证和检索性能证明其能在现实纵向数据中使用，同时筛查流程也揭示并纠正了真实数据中的身份错误。

Abstract: Patient identity errors can compromise longitudinal medical records, research databases, and downstream clinical decisions. We present a retinal biometric system for verifying claimed identities and retrieving the correct identity from color fundus images. We trained a 512-dimensional metric-learning encoder combining a ConvNeXtV2 backbone with ArcFace and triplet losses on 227,004 images from 21,851 patient-eye identities in the Rotterdam Study, spanning multiple imaging devices and up to 32.6 years of follow-up. The system was evaluated on held-out Rotterdam Study data and externally on the UK Biobank and Age-Related Eye Disease Study (AREDS). Before evaluation, we used the model to screen for identity inconsistencies and manually adjudicated flagged images, identifying incorrect assignments in 0.588% of Rotterdam Study images, 0.259% of UK Biobank images, and 0.164% of AREDS images. In retrospective-only verification after removing near-duplicate images, the system achieved AUROCs of 0.9998, 0.9997, and 0.9998 in the Rotterdam Study, UK Biobank, and AREDS, respectively. For identity retrieval using only previously acquired images, Recall@1 was 99.7%, 97.2%, and 97.6%, respectively, from galleries averaging 4436-8510 identities; the correct identity appeared among the top five results in at least 98.6% of cases. Performance remained robust across imaging devices and long follow-up intervals, while lower image quality and inconsistent retinal fields accounted for most failures. These findings establish retinal anatomy as a durable biometric signal, useful for safeguarding the integrity of longitudinal imaging records.

</details>


### [256] [One Adapter, Many Tasks: Task-Conditioned Feature Transformations for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31096)
*Yunxiang Fu,Meng Lou,Yizhou Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FACET是一种基于预训练模型的任务条件特征转换方法，用于类增量学习（CIL）。它学习单个共享适配器，通过动态任务条件特征转换将整体特征分布塑造成多个重叠减少的任务特定组件的混合，并提出一种无重放的任务条件特征一致性损失来缓解灾难性遗忘。在200个任务的长序列和20个任务的短序列上均取得优异性能，同时使用更少的可训练参数和GFLOPs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CIL方法存在局限：任务特定适配器参数量和计算效率低，而基于LoRA合并的方法使用静态聚合权重导致推理时特征表示干扰。作者旨在解决参数效率与特征判别性之间的权衡问题。

Method: FACET学习单一共享适配器，采用动态任务条件特征转换；提出无重放的任务条件特征一致性损失，用于缓解适配器特征空间中学习到的混合分布的灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在非常长的任务序列（200个任务）和标准短任务序列（20个任务）上均取得优于现有方法的性能，同时使用显著更少的可训练参数和GFLOPs。

Conclusion: FACET展示了仅使用单个适配器即可实现强缩放性的能力，在效率和性能之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Class-incremental learning (CIL) requires a model to incrementally learn tasks that contain new classes without accessing earlier training data while preserving the ability to recognize all seen classes. Recently, pretrained-model-based approaches have become prevalent by adapting a frozen backbone with additional lightweight trainable modules. Existing methods, however, exhibit limitations: task-specific adapters learn explicit per-task representations but are parameter- and computation-inefficient, while LoRA-based merging methods combine per-task LoRA parameters into a single model whose static aggregated weights cause representation interference during inference. To address these problems, we present \textbf{FACET}: task-conditioned \textbf{F}e\textbf{A}ture transformation with \textbf{C}ondition\textbf{E}d feature consis\textbf{T}ency, achieving excellent parameter efficiency while producing highly discriminative features during inference. When continually trained on a task sequence, FACET learns a single shared adapter that employs a dynamic task-conditioned feature transformation, shaping the overall feature distribution of the adapter into a mixture of overlap-reduced task-specific components. On the other hand, we propose an efficient replay-free task-conditioned feature consistency loss, aiming to mitigate catastrophic forgetting of the learned mixture distribution in the adapter's feature space. Even when maintaining only a single adapter, FACET demonstrates robust scalability. On both very long task sequences (e.g., 200 tasks) and standard short task sequences (e.g., 20 tasks), our method achieves superior performance while using significantly fewer trainable parameters and GFLOPs. The code will be made open source upon acceptance.

</details>


### [257] [DreamX-Creator: Democratizing Native Audio-Video Generation at 2K Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31106)
*Jiashu Zhu,Yanhao Zheng,Ruitian Tian,Rujing Dang,Shen Zhang,Bingze Song,Jiachen Lei,Ruimin Lin,Jiahong Wu,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamX-Creator 1.0 是一个以7B生成器为核心的紧凑型原生联合音视频生成系统，基于首帧和文本提示联合去噪音视频流，通过门控跨模态注意力耦合双流，并配套统一数据系统、渐进式联合训练、模态感知强化学习和自回归1步2K精修流水线，在性能上与开源SOTA竞争的同时实现紧凑高效的部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成器通常省略音频或使用独立阶段合成音频，导致视觉动态与声学事件之间缺乏互惠建模，难以生成原生同步的音视频内容。作者希望构建一个紧凑、可访问的统一音视频生成系统，打破音视频分离生成的局限。

Method: 核心架构为7B生成器，前半个网络独立处理音视频流，后半个网络通过Gated Cross-Modal Attention耦合，其token级和head级输出门控调制每个活跃的跨模态注意力head。训练方面包含：统一音视频数据系统（构建过滤时间连贯片段、结构化多模态标注、能力导向数据池）、渐进式联合训练（两个音视频预训练阶段+高质量微调）、音视频强化学习（模态感知多模态反馈路由到对应流）。高分辨率输出采用Autoregressive 1-Step 2K Refinement：将双向多步教师适配为自回归多步精修器，再蒸馏为每个时间块只需一次去噪评估的学生模型。

Result: DreamX-Creator 1.0实现了原生、同步的音视频生成，性能与开源SOTA系统竞争，并已开源紧凑的7B生成器和2K精修器以促进领域普及。

Conclusion: 通过紧凑的7B模型实现原生联合音视频生成，证明了在较小规模下仍能达到竞争性性能，为统一音视频生成建模提供了可访问的研究基础。

Abstract: Recent video generators often omit audio or synthesize it in a separate stage, limiting reciprocal modeling of visual dynamics and acoustic events. We present DreamX-Creator 1.0, a compact native joint audio-video generation system centered on a 7B generator. Conditioned on a first frame and a text prompt, the generator jointly denoises modality-specialized audio and video streams. The streams are processed independently in the first half of the network and coupled in the latter half through Gated Cross-Modal Attention, whose token- and head-wise output gates modulate each active cross-modal attention-head output. A unified Audio-Video Data System constructs and filters temporally coherent clips, produces structured multimodal annotations, and organizes clips into capability-oriented data pools. Progressive Joint Training comprises two audio-video pre-training stages followed by High-Quality Finetuning. Audio-Video Reinforcement Learning further post-trains the generator with Modality-Aware Multimodal Feedback that routes video-, audio-, and cross-modal feedback to the corresponding streams. For high-resolution output, our Autoregressive 1-Step 2K Refinement pipeline adapts a bidirectional multi-step teacher into an autoregressive multi-step refiner and distills it into a student requiring one denoising evaluation per temporal chunk. Overall, DreamX-Creator 1.0 achieves native, synchronized audio-video generation with performance competitive with state-of-the-art open-source systems. By releasing our compact 7B generator and 2K Refiner, we seek to democratize native audio-video generation and provide an accessible foundation for future research in unified audio-video generative modeling.

</details>


### [258] [VeriCam: A Verification Baseline for the Classification of Unknown Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31107)
*Lucas Wojcik,Gabriel E. Lima,Sergio M. Silva,Eduil Nascimento,David Menotti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VeriCam是一个用于零样本细粒度分类的流程，通过验证任务训练图像模型构建关系图，再利用图聚类算法（朴素算法或Leiden算法）对未知类别的数据进行分类。在LPLCv2真实交通监控数据集上，针对跨设备场景达到93.45的F1分数和80.13的V-Measure分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在零样本分类中虽然泛化能力强，但在需要基于细微特征区分细粒度类别的真实任务（如车牌识别）中表征能力不足。现有文献缺乏利用验证任务学习细粒度特征并进行未知类别分类的方法。

Method: 1) 利用验证任务（判别图像对是否属于同一类别）训练图像模型，使其学习细粒度细节的表征空间；2) 基于成对判别构建数据点之间的关系图；3) 提出两种图聚类方法：朴素聚类算法和适配的Leiden图聚类算法；4) 在LPLCv2交通监控数据集上验证，并动态识别采集设备偏差以构建公平基准。

Result: 在跨设备场景下，验证基线达到93.45 F1分数，聚类步骤达到80.13 V-Measure分数。数据集存在固有的采集设备偏差，该方法通过标签无关方式动态识别设备来源。

Conclusion: VeriCam展示了通过验证任务学习细粒度特征并结合图聚类可以有效处理真实场景中的零样本细粒度分类问题，同时揭示并解决了交通监控数据中的设备偏差问题。

Abstract: The advent of foundation models have enabled a new era in zero-shot classification. Yet, key challenges persist. Despite their impressive generalization power that leverages the immense pre-training knowledge, both foundation models for image and text as well as vision-text hybrids lack the representational power needed for fine-grained, minutiae-based class separation that some real-world tasks require. To address the current gaps in the literature, we propose VeriCam, a pipeline designed to learn highly specialized features that enable classification of unknown classes in unseen data. VeriCam works by leveraging the representation power of image models trained for the verification task, where the model develops an intricate feature space that incorporates fine-grained details. By training a model to discriminate between pairs of images from the same and different classes, a relational graph is constructed, representing the class relationships between data points. We then present two approaches for graph clustering: a naive algorithm and a specific setup for the Leiden graph clustering algorithm. The pipeline is validated on the LPLCv2 dataset, which comprises real-world traffic surveillance images. We show that the dataset carries an inherent capture device bias that is posed as a generalization challenge for downstream License Plate recognition tasks such as OCR. As such, we dynamically identify capture devices with a label-agnostic approach, enabling the construction of a fair and unbiased benchmark. In the cross-device scenario, our pipeline reaches an F1-Score of 93.45 in the verification baseline and a V-Measure score of 80.13 in the clustering step. All code is publicly available at https://github.com/lmlwojcik/VeriCam

</details>


### [259] [BLARM: Animating 3D Objects from Video via Blending Latent Rigid Motion Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31113)
*Pradyumn Goyal,Yizhak Ben-Shabat,Hsueh-Ti Derek Liu,Haomiao Jiang,Snehasish Mukherjee,Kyle Spence,Mark Stauber,Evangelos Kalogerakis,Yunze Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BLARM是一个前馈式视频驱动3D网格动画方法，无需显式骨架、蒙皮权重或绑定注释，通过学习一组紧凑的时间变化刚体运动分量和时间不变的顶点到组件蒙皮权重来预测与视频运动一致的时间连贯动画网格，并采用分解时空注意力与运动感知对比学习进行训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频驱动3D网格动画方法要么依赖显式骨骼绑定（rig），要么直接回归高维顶点运动，这两类方法分别受限于需要人工标注/绑定流程，或产生的高维变形空间难以保持时间一致性和可解释性。作者希望在不依赖骨架、笼子、蒙皮权重或绑定注释的情况下，从单目视频中恢复紧凑且可解释的运动结构。

Method: 1) 将动画表示为时间变化刚体运动分量 + 时间不变的顶点到组件蒙皮权重，形成低维变形空间；2) 通过分解的时空注意力机制，将几何派生的变形隐变量与视频特征进行条件化；3) 解码器将预测的蒙皮权重混合的刚体变换，生成最终动画网格；4) 训练目标包括轨迹重建损失、熵正则化和运动感知对比学习。

Result: BLARM在单目视频驱动的3D网格动画上生成了准确且时间稳定的动画，同时恢复出紧凑、可解释的运动结构，无需显式骨骼/绑定注释。

Conclusion: BLARM展示了一种免绑定、低维度的视频驱动3D动画框架，通过学习紧凑的刚体运动分量和蒙皮权重实现时间连贯的网格动画，在不需要手工注释的前提下兼顾准确性与运动结构的可解释性。

Abstract: We introduce BLARM, a feed-forward method for video-driven 3D mesh animation. Given a monocular video and a static object mesh, BLARM predicts a temporally coherent animated mesh whose motion follows the video. Rather than relying on explicit rigs or directly regressing high-dimensional vertex motion, we represent animation using a compact set of learned, time-varying rigid motion components and time-invariant vertex-to-component skinning weights. This yields a low-dimensional deformation space without requiring skeletons, cages, skinning weights, or rig annotations. Our architecture conditions geometry-derived deformation latents on video features through factorized spatial-temporal attention, then decodes rigid transformations blended by predicted skinning weights. Trained with trajectory reconstruction, entropy regularization, and motion-aware contrastive learning, BLARM produces accurate and temporally stable animations while recovering compact, interpretable motion structure from monocular video.

</details>


### [260] [BRF-GS: Hyperspectral Bidirectional Reflectance Factor Modeling and Image Generation Based on 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31159)
*Yiling Yao,Wenjuan Zhang,Bowen Wang,Bocheng Li,Wentao Song,Bing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BRF-GS，一个基于3D Gaussian Splatting的框架，用于双向反射因子（BRF）建模和多角度高光谱反射率图像生成。通过混合BRDF驱动核、几何可靠波段选择和两阶段训练策略，解决了现有3D辐射传输模型计算密集和高光谱数据高维性问题，并构建了AIR-BRF多角度高光谱方向反射数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D辐射传输模型需要复杂场景构建和计算密集的求解器，难以高效生成多角度高光谱反射率图像。3DGS虽然高效，但其低阶球谐表示不足以表达复杂方向反射，且高光谱数据的高维性和波段间质量差异带来额外挑战。

Method: BRF-GS包含三个核心组件：(1)混合BRDF驱动核用于表示复杂方向反射特性；(2)选择几何可靠的光谱波段进行稳健的3D场景初始化；(3)两阶段训练策略，将几何优化与光谱建模解耦。同时构建了包含三个场景（多样的自然和人工目标）的AIR-BRF数据集。

Result: 实验表明BRF-GS在空间和光谱保真度上优于现有方法，并能准确复现特征性的视角依赖BRF响应。

Conclusion: 该方法为遥感场景中的BRF建模和多角度高光谱反射率图像生成提供了高效的数据驱动途径。

Abstract: The bidirectional reflectance factor (BRF) characterizes the directional radiative properties of terrestrial surfaces. However, existing three-dimensional (3D) radiative transfer models require complex scene construction and computationally intensive radiative transfer solvers, limiting efficient generation of multi-angle hyperspectral reflectance imagery. 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) offers an efficient framework for neural scene representation and novel view synthesis, but its low-order spherical harmonics representation is insufficient for complex directional reflectance, while the high dimensionality and inter-band quality differences of hyperspectral data introduce additional challenges. To address these challenges, we propose BRF-GS, a 3DGS-based framework for BRF modeling and hyperspectral reflectance image generation. BRF-GS introduces a hybrid BRDF-driven kernel to represent complex directional reflectance, selects geometry-reliable spectral bands for robust 3D scene initialization, and adopts a two-stage training strategy that decouples geometry optimization from spectral modeling. We further construct the AIR-BRF dataset, a multi-angle hyperspectral directional reflectance dataset comprising three scenes with diverse natural and artificial targets. Experiments demonstrate that BRF-GS achieves superior spatial and spectral fidelity and accurately reproduces characteristic view-dependent BRF responses. The proposed framework provides an efficient data-driven approach for BRF modeling and multi-angle hyperspectral reflectance image generation in remote sensing scenes.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [261] [RedLight-VLA: Models for traffic-rule grounding and behavioral emphasis in driving policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28656)
*Bala Murali Manoghar Sai Sudhakar,Sourab Bapu Sridhar,Sandipan Das,Rahul Ahuja,Meda Lazar,Ashish Garg,Pratik Likhar,Senthil Yogamani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RedLight-VLA 通过行为重加权（BR）和并行辅助头（AUX）两种训练目标，无需额外人工规则标注，显著改善行为克隆VLA驾驶策略在信号交叉口的红绿灯停车线行为，降低越线率和停车线速度误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆的VLA驾驶策略在信号交叉口的红绿灯规则性操控（如刹车、起步）上表现不佳，因为这些动作在平均轨迹损失中占比低，且融合表示缺乏对红绿灯和停车线状态的显式监督。

Method: 1) 行为重加权（BR）：利用专家未来轨迹，通过旋转不变的纵向动力学和保尺度降维，强调稀有的减速/加速样本，禁用时能精确恢复基线。 2) 并行辅助头（AUX）：在融合后的连续规则token上监督交通灯和停车线状态，无需自回归语言生成或改动轨迹解码器。

Result: 红灯停车线越线率从7.3%降至6.8%，停车线速度误差降低12.7%，3秒交通灯切片的ADE/FDE从0.274/0.964m改善到0.247/0.897m。绿灯误停从3.2%增至3.9%（但组合BR+AUX缓解了AUX单独4.0%的增幅）。非交通灯ADE/FDE也从0.268/0.956m改善到0.241/0.876m。

Conclusion: 基于轨迹的行为重加权和基于辅助头的规则状态监督可以互补地改善VLA驾驶策略的交通灯规则遵守行为，且不增加人工规则标注或推理开销。

Abstract: Behavior-cloned Vision-Language-Action (VLA) driving policies struggle with rare rule-governed maneuvers at signalized intersections. Braking and launching examples contribute little to averaged trajectory loss, while fused representations lack explicit supervision for the governing traffic-light and stop-line state. We present RedLight-VLA, a training objective that uses expert futures and automatically generated perception targets without additional manual rule annotation. First, trajectory-derived behavioral reweighting (BR) emphasizes rare deceleration and acceleration using rotation-invariant longitudinal dynamics and a scale-preserving reduction that exactly recovers the baseline when disabled. Second, parallel auxiliary (AUX) heads ground traffic-light and stop-line state in continuous post-fusion rule tokens, without autoregressive language generation or changes to the trajectory decoder. We evaluate on a curated set of 20 s sequences with a 5 s prediction horizon. Controlled variants share the same backbone, training data, decoder, and evaluation population. Against an otherwise identical VLA baseline, RedLight-VLA reduces red-light stop-line overshoot from 7.3% to6.8%, reduces stop-line velocity error by 12.7%, and improves 3 s trafficlight-sliced ADE/FDE from 0.274/0.964 m to 0.247/0.897 m. Green-light false stops increase from 3.2% to 3.9%; however, combining BR with AUX supervision mitigates the larger increase observed for AUX alone (4.0%). The combined model also improves non-traffic-light ADE/FDE from 0.268/0.956 m to 0.241/0.876 m and outperforms either mechanism alone on all four sliced displacement measures.

</details>


### [262] [The Potential of Haptic Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28664)
*Jianquan Wang,Haiwei Dong,Abdulmotaleb El Saddik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出触觉基础模型（HFMs）的概念框架，探讨将被动的大语言模型和视觉语言模型过渡为主动触觉基础模型所需的范式转变，涵盖动作耦合、物理动力学表征空间、连续时间序列数据粒度、动作条件未来状态预测四个维度，并在TacBench上评测UniTouch、AnyTouch、T3和Sparsh等触觉模型在力估计、滑移检测和相对位姿估计上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在语言和视觉领域已取得巨大成功，但在具身AI中的扩展受限于缺乏泛化的触觉感知能力。消费电子设备（智能手机、可穿戴设备、VR控制器、家用机器人、健康监测）需要安全自适应的物理交互，而现有触觉模型因硬件异构性和主动物理数据采集的必要性而固化为任务特定，亟需统一的基础模型框架来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 文章提出Haptic Foundation Models（HFMs）概念框架，围绕四个核心维度阐述从被动LLM/VLM到主动HFM的范式转型：动作耦合（行动与感知的闭环）、物理动力学表征空间、连续时间序列数据粒度、动作条件的未来状态预测。同时整合现有大规模触觉数据集，并在TacBench基准上对比评测UniTouch、AnyTouch、T3和Sparsh四种代表性触觉模型。

Result: 系统梳理了触觉基础模型的发展路径，明确了HFMs的四大核心设计维度，整合并评测了现有主流触觉模型在标准基准TacBench上的力估计、滑移检测和相对位姿估计三项任务的表现，揭示了当前触觉模型与通用基础模型之间的差距及未来发展方向。

Conclusion: 触觉基础模型是迈向通用具身AI的关键方向，需要从被动语言/视觉基础模型转向主动的、物理交互驱动的HFMs。当前触觉模型虽在特定任务上表现良好，但缺乏跨硬件迁移和泛化能力，未来需在动作耦合、物理表征和时序建模等维度持续突破。

Abstract: Despite the success of foundation models in language and vision, their expansion into embodied AI is bottlenecked by a lack of generalized touch sensing. This limitation is especially relevant to consumer electronics, where smartphones, wearables, VR controllers, home robots, and health monitoring devices require safe and adaptive physical interaction. Constrained by hardware heterogeneity and the necessity of active physical data collection, current haptic models remain rigidly task-specific. To overcome these limitations, this article explores the transformative potential and developmental trajectory of Haptic Foundation Models (HFMs). We detail the paradigm shift required to transition from passive Large Language Models and Vision Language Models into active HFMs across four core dimensions: action coupling, physical dynamical representation space, continuous time-series data granularity, and action-conditioned future state prediction. Furthermore, we synthesize existing large-scale tactile datasets and benchmark UniTouch, AnyTouch, T3, and Sparsh on TacBench for force estimation, slip detection, and relative pose estimation.

</details>


### [263] [Cognitively-Grounded On-Device Runtime Learning for Ground Robots in Unknown Physical Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28677)
*Yihao Cai,Yanbing Mao,Christian Lebiere*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CogRun 是一个面向安全关键地面机器人的框架，支持其在未知物理环境中完全在边缘AI设备上进行认知驱动的运行时学习，无需先验地图或感知知识。框架由 Learning-Agent（认知神经学习架构，融合actor-critic RL与IBL）、Rational-Agent（非学习安全关键模块）和 Coordinator（协调两者）构成，已在四足机器人真实野外森林和越野车模拟野外森林场景中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键地面机器人在未知环境中运行时，需要不断学习以适应环境，但传统方法依赖先验地图、云端计算或无线通信，在无连接或有限连接环境中难以部署。如何在边缘设备上实现安全且高效的运行时学习是核心挑战。

Method: CogRun 由三部分组成：Learning-Agent 采用认知神经学习架构，包含专用回放缓冲区、认知驱动的经验采样、以及演员-评论家RL与实例学习(IBL)的安全感知动作混合；Rational-Agent 作为非学习模块专门处理安全关键功能；Coordinator 管理两者的交互以促进安全和高效的运行时学习。完整自主体栈（感知、学习、控制）部署于边缘AI设备。

Result: 在真实世界野外森林中的四足机器人以及模拟野外森林中的越野自动驾驶车辆上进行了实验，结果表明 CogRun 能够实现安全和高效的运行时学习，使机器人在复杂未知环境中安全地持续与物理世界交互，提升任务性能。

Conclusion: CogRun 证明了在边缘AI设备上无需无线通信即可实现安全关键地面机器人的运行时学习，拓展了在无连接挑战性环境中的机器人应用范围，并为认知驱动的安全运行时学习提供了可行框架。

Abstract: This paper presents \ul{CogRun}, a framework that enables safety-critical ground robots to perform cognitively-grounded runtime learning entirely on edge-AI devices in unknown physical environments, without prior maps or perceptual knowledge. CogRun consists of three components: a Learning-Agent, a Rational-Agent, and a Coordinator. The Learning-Agent is novel in cognitive-neural learning architecture, which featurs dedicated replay buffers, cognition-driven experience sampling, and a safety-aware action blending of actor-critic reinforcement learning (RL) with instance-based learning (IBL). The Rational-Agent is a non-learning module that complements the Learning-Agent by exclusively handling safety-critical functions, while the Coordinator manages interactions between the two agents to promote safe and efficient runtime learning. CogRun's full autonomy stack (i.e., perception, learning, and control) on edge-AI devices eliminates dependence on wireless communications, enabling broader applications in challenging environments with limited or no connectivity. Experiments on a quadruped robot in real-world wild forests and on an off-road autonomous vehicle in a simulated wild forest demonstrate that CogRun enables safe and efficient runtime learning, allowing robots to safely and continuously interact with the physical world for enhancing task performance in complex, unknown environments.

</details>


### [264] [RoboGesture: Real-Time Semantic-aligned Co-Speech Gestures Generation for Humanoid Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28693)
*Zifan Wang,Ziang Ren,Pengyang Shi,Zirui Wang,Chenghuai Lin,Tianze Wang,Zekun Qi,Liangliang Zhao,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboGesture是一个面向人形机器人的端到端手势生成框架，通过协同设计数据集（300+手势类别）、建模（层级语义-声学对齐器 + 基于扩散Transformer的条件流匹配生成器）与控制（MPC安全滤波器），实现机器人实时倾听、响应并生成语义匹配、物理安全的同步手势。在Unitree G1人形机器人上验证了安全性、节奏感和语义适当性均优于SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人要自然地与人类交互，需对语音产生同步且语义有意义的手势。但该任务面临三大障碍：语义丰富的数据集稀缺、"模态遮蔽"（模型忽视音频线索而依赖运动惯性）、以及物理安全层面的sim-to-real差距。

Method: 1) 建立RoboGesture数据集（300+手势类别），并开发自动化管道合成大规模无碰撞、机器人专属的音频-动作对；2) 提出层级语义-声学对齐器（HSA），从原始音频token中提取多粒度韵律与语义线索；3) 基于扩散Transformer+条件流匹配的流式条件运动生成器；4) Anti-Inertia CFG Masking防止模型坍缩为重复历史模式，迫使模型主动挖掘音频模态的控制信号；5) MPC安全滤波器确保物理硬件上的实时无碰撞执行。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上的实验表明，RoboGesture相比SOTA基线生成更安全、更有节奏感、语义更恰当的手势响应，且系统可实时完整运行（机器人倾听、回应、手势同步）。

Conclusion: RoboGesture通过数据-建模-控制的协同设计，系统性地解决了人形机器人语音驱动手势生成的数据稀缺、模态遮蔽和安全执行三大难题，验证了在实际人形平台上运行完整交互闭环的可行性。

Abstract: Enabling humanoid robots to respond to human speech with synchronized and semantically meaningful gestures is fundamental to natural human-robot interaction. However, this task faces three critical barriers: the scarcity of semantically rich datasets, the "modality eclipse" where models ignore audio cues in favor of kinematic inertia, and the sim-to-real gap regarding physical safety. We propose RoboGesture, a robot-centric framework that co-designs data, modeling, and control to power a complete interactive human-humanoid system in which the robot listens, responds, and gestures in real time. We first establish the RoboGesture dataset featuring over 300 gesture categories and develop an automated pipeline to synthesize large-scale collision-free, robot-specific audio-motion pairs. Our architecture features a Hierarchical Semantic-Acoustic Aligner that extracts multi-granular prosodic and semantic cues directly from raw audio tokens. These cues drive a Streaming Conditional Motion Generator based on a diffusion transformer with conditional flow matching. To ensure high responsiveness, we introduce Anti-Inertia CFG Masking, which prevents the model from collapsing into repetitive historical patterns by compelling it to proactively mine control signals from the audio modality. Finally, an MPC-based safety filter ensures real-time, collision-free execution on physical hardware. Experiments on a Unitree G1 humanoid demonstrate that RoboGesture generates safer, more rhythmic, and more semantically appropriate responses compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [265] [Multi-Group Pipe Routing under Permanent Geometric Occupancy: Problem, Benchmark, and Classical Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28697)
*Deng Quan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究增材制造下的多组管道布线问题——通道一旦放置即永久占用体积，区别于经典多智能体路径规划(MAPF)中智能体移动后释放空间。作者提出几何双重见证冲突的形式化定义、可控难度的3D基准测试集（两个走廊生成器×两个障碍画笔，含可行性见证），并给出CBS、PBS和优先级规划三种经典算法的适配基线。实验在固定时间预算下以成功率评估，PlaneSlice困难实例显示CBS明显优于PBS和PP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增材制造使液压元件内部流体通道可沿自由3D路径排布，典型场景是旋转直驱伺服阀中的多组通道布局。由于通道一旦放置就永久占据体积，后续通道必须避让已有几何，这与经典MAPF的"移动后释放空间"假设根本不同。作者希望为该新型耦合约束设置提供正式的问题定义、可复现基准及经典基线，而非提出新的最优MAPF算法。

Method: 1) 将问题形式化为多组管道布线（multi-group pipe routing under permanent geometric occupancy），引入几何双重见证冲突（geometric dual-witness conflicts）；2) 构造3D基准：两个走廊生成器×两个障碍画笔，控制难度并提供可行性见证；3) 将经典CBS、PBS、优先级规划(PP)适配到该耦合问题；4) 在固定时间预算下以成功率进行评估。

Result: 在PlaneSlice困难实例中，CBS的成功率明显高于PBS和优先级规划；在较容易的单元上各方法表现验证了流水线的有效性。整体提供了可复现的问题定义、测试套件和经典基线结果。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于正式的问题形式化、可控难度的3D基准和经典算法基线，目的是为后续研究提供可复现的评估基准。作者明确声明不提出新的最优MAPF算法，定位是基准/基线型工作。

Abstract: Additive manufacturing (AM) enables compact hydraulic components whose internal fluid channels can follow free-form 3D paths rather than conventionally drilled holes. A representative case is multi-group channel layout in rotary direct-drive servo valves: once a channel is placed it permanently occupies volume, so later channels must clear earlier geometry--unlike classical multi-agent pathfinding (MAPF), where agents free space after moving. We study this setting as multi-group pipe routing under permanent geometric occupancy. Our contributions are a problem formalization with geometric dual-witness conflicts, a constructive 3D benchmark (two corridor generators x two obstacle painters, controlled difficulty, feasibility witnesses), and baseline results for CBS, PBS, and priority planning adapted to this coupling. We evaluate by success rate under a fixed time budget. PlaneSlice hard instances clearly rank CBS above PBS and PP, while easier cells validate the pipeline. The goal is a reproducible problem definition, suite, and classical baselines--not a new optimal MAPF algorithm.

</details>


### [266] [RoboPhys-3D: A Comprehensive Embodied World Model Evaluation via 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28718)
*Tianyi Wang,Jiazhou Chen,Yiming Xu,Xiangyu Li,Tianyi Zeng,Chih-Hsien Chou,Ning Lu,Liang Peng,Junfeng Jiao,Christian Claudel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboPhys-3D 是一个基于 RoboTwin 2.0 构建的 3D 接地具身世界模型基准，覆盖 50 个操作任务、5,000 个回合和 25,000 段多视角真值视频。其核心特点是生成视频和真值视频通过相同的 3D 重建管线处理，从而区分重建诱导误差与生成诱导误差。基准组织 50 个指标到 4 个层次，引入 Average Full Score 和 RoboPhyscore 两个评分体系。在四个代表性视频世界模型中，Cosmos 3 取得最高 RoboPhyscore (0.6330)，但与人类评估高度一致，揭示了感知级评估无法捕捉的执行级失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统具身世界模型基准缺乏统一的 3D 接地协议，无法验证生成的视频是否保持底层 3D 场景状态或可转化为可执行动作，导致评估体系与真实具身能力脱节。

Method: 基于 RoboTwin 2.0 构建 50 个操作任务跨四种机制的基准；让生成视频与真值视频经过同一个 3D 重建管线以区分重建误差与生成误差；设计 50 个互补指标、18 个子维度、4 个层次（像素保真、3D 几何一致性、状态级理解、任务级完整性）；提出 Average Full Score 和 RoboPhyscore 两个评分体系。

Result: Cosmos 3 达到最高 RoboPhyscore 0.6330（真值的 92.7%）；状态和执行接地指标揭示了感知级和 VLM 判断无法捕捉的显著失败；RoboPhyscore 与人类评估强一致（Pearson r=0.9761，Spearman ρ=0.8962）。

Conclusion: RoboPhyscore 证明了接地、执行感知评估对具身世界模型能力评估的重要性，为 EWM 评估提供了新的 3D 接地协议标准。

Abstract: Video world models increasingly serve as data engines, action planners, and simulators for embodied AI, but conventional embodied world model (EWM) benchmarks lack a unified 3D-grounded protocol for establishing whether generated rollouts preserve the underlying 3D scene state or translate into executable actions. We introduce RoboPhys-3D, a 3D-grounded EWM benchmark built on RoboTwin 2.0, covering 50 manipulation tasks across four regimes, with 5,000 episodes and 25,000 multi-view ground-truth videos. A defining feature of RoboPhys-3D is that generated and ground-truth videos are processed through the same 3D reconstruction pipeline, enabling reconstruction-induced error to be distinguished from generation-induced error. The RoboPhys-3D benchmark organizes 50 complementary metrics into 18 sub-dimensions across four levels: pixel-level fidelity, 3D geometry consistency, state-level understanding, and task-level completeness. We further introduce Average Full Score, a hierarchical score averaging all 50 metrics for comprehensive evaluation, and RoboPhyscore, a compact task-aligned score averaging the metrics most strongly correlated with task success. Among the four representative video world models, Cosmos 3 achieves the highest RoboPhyscore (0.6330, 92.7% of ground truth), while state- and execution-grounded metrics reveal substantial failures that perceptual and vision-language model-based judgments fail to capture. RoboPhyscore further exhibits strong agreement with human evaluation (Pearson r = 0.9761 and Spearman \r{ho} = 0.8962), demonstrating the importance of grounded, execution-aware evaluation for EWM capability.

</details>


### [267] [Vehicle Drift Emergence: Continuous Evolution from Grip Driving to the Handling Limit via Boundary Exploration Learning Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28723)
*Sheng Zhao,Binh-Minh Nguyen,Hangyu Lu,Xiaodong Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出边界探索学习模型预测控制器（BE-LMPC），在循环计时赛最小化任务中无需显式漂移参考，通过迭代优化空间速度分配和构建经验安全集，使车辆在接近轮胎极限时自发涌现漂移状态。实验表明在摩擦系数0.6下，圈速从49.95s提升至25.50s，漂移在第11圈首次出现，最大侧偏角达16.5°；而在高摩擦系数场景下无漂移发生，证明漂移是极限操控的条件性延续而非独立运动模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动漂移控制器通常预设漂移平衡点或侧偏角参考，只解决如何执行漂移的问题，而未解决从抓地行驶到漂移的连续过渡问题。作者希望在无显式漂移参考的条件下，探索漂移何时以及如何作为极限操控的自然涌现行为出现。

Method: 采用边界探索学习模型预测控制器（BE-LMPC），核心组件包括：1）从已完成圈次构建经验安全集，保证状态可恢复性；2）使用局部移位终端代价函数，引导迭代性能提升；3）在固定全局速度约束下迭代优化空间速度分配。使用combined-slip Fiala轮胎模型，分析验证了从附着到滑移转换过程中轮胎力与车辆状态的连续性条件。

Result: 在摩擦系数0.6下，圈速从第3圈的49.95s降至第12圈的25.50s，漂移在第11圈首次涌现，第12圈达到16.5°侧偏角和0.894后轴利用率。对比摩擦系数0.8-1.2场景下无漂移发生，摩擦系数1.2时可达到相似峰值速度但后轴利用率仅0.483，证明漂移仅在性能需求逼近轮胎能力时才涌现。

Conclusion: 漂移应被理解为极限操控的条件性延续——当增加性能需求逼近可用轮胎容量时自然出现，而非单独预设的运动模式。该发现为自动驾驶极限操控的连续控制提供了理论框架，减少了漂移与正常驾驶之间的模式切换需求。

Abstract: Automated drift controllers commonly track a prescribed drift equilibrium, sideslip reference, or trajectory. These formulations establish how to execute drift, whereas the continuous transition from grip driving to drift near the handling limit remains unresolved. This paper defines drift emergence in a repetitive lap time minimization task, where neither the controller objective nor the reward contains an explicit drift reference. A boundary exploration learning model predictive controller (BE-LMPC) constructs an empirical safe set and a locally shifted terminal cost from completed laps. By iteratively improving spatial speed allocation under a fixed global speed bound, the controller progressively explores larger sideslip and yaw rate envelopes while preserving recoverability. As lap performance improves, sustained sideslip and pronounced yaw motion emerge while the rear axle approaches saturation. Analysis shows that, when external conditions vary smoothly, the transition from tire adhesion to sliding does not itself cause abrupt changes in tire force or vehicle state. The combined-slip Fiala model satisfies this continuity condition at the transition. At a tire road friction coefficient of 0.6, lap time decreases from 49.95 s on Lap~3 to 25.50 s on Lap~12, with drift first emerging on Lap~11. Lap~12 reaches 16.5$^\circ$ sideslip and 0.894 rear axle utilization. In contrast, no drift is detected for friction coefficients from 0.8 to 1.2; at 1.2, a similar peak speed is achieved with only 0.483 rear axle utilization. These results characterize drift as a conditional continuation of limit handling that emerges when increasing performance demand approaches the available tire capacity, rather than as a separately prescribed motion mode.

</details>


### [268] [Generation of High-Level Concepts in 3D Scene Graphs via Autoregressive Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28733)
*Jose Andres Millan-Romera,Samuel Cognolato,Holger Voos,Jose Luis Sanchez-Lopez,Luciano Serafini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一的自回归扩散图生成模型，能联合学习图结构与节点特征，从观测的垂直平面自底向上构建任意深度层次的室内3D场景图（3DSG），无需针对特定概念类别的手工规则或分离的图结构/特征模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典3DSG高层概念生成依赖针对特定类别的手工规则，而学习方法需要为图结构与空间节点特征分别建模，限制了向新类别和更复杂层次结构的扩展性。

Method: 提出统一的自回归扩散式图生成模型，联合学习图结构和节点特征，以垂直平面为观测输入，自底向上地构建任意层次深度的完整3DSG。

Result: 在合成场景、真实建筑平面图和机器人传感器数据等3DSG数据集上，方法一致超越所有学习基线和随机基线；在最大层次和真实单层数据上超越具备目标图大小oracle访问的one-shot模型。此外提出基于Fused Gromov-Wasserstein距离的图级评估指标。

Conclusion: 统一的自回归扩散图生成模型能够高效且可扩展地生成任意层次深度的3DSG，并提供了原则性的图级评估方法，为机器人感知与SLAM的增量空间推理提供了新的生成式方案。

Abstract: Indoor 3D Scene Graphs (3DSGs) represent environments as multi-layer hierarchies that connect observed geometric primitives (e.g., planes) to higher-level metric-semantic concepts (e.g., rooms, floors, buildings), enabling incremental spatial reasoning for robotic perception and SLAM. However, classical high-level concept generation approaches rely on hand-crafted rules for specific concept classes, while learning-based methods require separate models for graph structure and spatial node features (e.g., centroids), which limits scalability to novel classes and more complex hierarchies. We propose a unified autoregressive diffusion-based graph generative model that jointly learns structure and features, constructing complete 3DSGs bottom-up from observed vertical planes across arbitrary hierarchy depths. Our method consistently surpasses all learning-based and random baselines across 3DSG datasets spanning synthetic scenes, real architectural floor plans, and robotic sensor data, with varying layout complexity and hierarchy depth, and surpasses a one-shot model with oracle access to the target graph size on the largest hierarchy and on real single-floor data. Finally, we propose an adaptation of the Fused Gromov--Wasserstein distance for principled graph-level evaluation of generated 3DSGs against ground truth.

</details>


### [269] [Adversarial Calibration Attack on Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28778)
*Liangkai Liu,Qingzhao Zhang,Kang G. Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗性标定攻击（ACA），首次针对自动驾驶中相机-激光雷达在线标定系统发起物理攻击。通过单张对抗性海报，同时欺骗误标定检测器触发标定流程，并引导标定估计器输出错误的变换参数。在KITTI、nuScenes等基准上可诱导高达33.9度的平均旋转标定误差，在CARLA仿真中可造成碰撞，并在真实Husky机器人的物理实验中成功复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶攻击大多假设传感器标定正确，但实际中在线标定会因振动、温度或位移发生漂移。作者识别出在线标定是一个被忽视的新攻击平面——一次被篡改的标定更新会持续影响后续所有融合操作，从感知传播到规划与控制，具有系统级安全影响。

Method: ACA使用单张对抗海报，通过统一优化框架联合设计海报的几何形状与纹理，同时实现两个目标：(1) 欺骗误标定检测器使其误判需要触发标定过程；(2) 引导标定估计器收敛到错误的变换参数。

Result: 在KITTI和nuScenes基准上诱导最高33.9度平均旋转标定误差，严重降低目标检测性能；在CARLA仿真中，攻击者构建的漏洞场景下被篡改的标定被接受后导致碰撞；在真实Husky机器人上，打印的对抗海报成功复现标定误差。

Conclusion: 在线标定是自动驾驶实际且安全关键的攻击面。该工作揭示了现有在线标定系统在对抗环境下的脆弱性，为自动驾驶系统的安全设计提供了新的警示方向和防御需求。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) rely on accurate camera-LiDAR calibration for multimodal sensor fusion. In practice, calibration can drift due to vibration, temperature variation, or minor sensor displacement, motivating online calibration algorithms that detect and correct misalignment at runtime while allowing the vehicle to continue operating without a factory visit. Existing AV attacks largely assume correct calibration. We instead identify online sensor calibration as a new attack plane. A corrupted calibration update can persist across subsequent fusion operations, causing system-wide errors that propagate from perception to planning and control. We present Adversarial Calibration Attack (ACA), the first physical attack against camera-LiDAR online calibration. Using a single adversarial poster, ACA first spoofs the miscalibration detector to trigger the calibration process and then steers the calibration estimator toward an incorrect transformation. A unified optimization jointly designs the poster's geometry and texture for both objectives. We evaluate ACA across benchmark datasets, simulation, and physical experiments. On benchmark datasets such as KITTI and nuScenes, ACA induces up to 33.9 degrees mean rotational calibration error, thereby severely degrading object detection. In the CARLA simulator, the attack causes a collision when the corrupted calibration is accepted in vulnerable scenarios crafted by the attacker. On a real Husky robot, a printed adversarial poster successfully reproduces the calibration error. These results demonstrate that online calibration is a practical and safety-critical attack surface for AVs.

</details>


### [270] [Dual Park-Ravani Interpolation of Rigid Motions: Acceleration-Field Continuity and Holonomic Hermite Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28826)
*Daniel Condurache*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将Park-Ravani样条构造从SO(3)群转移到正交对偶张量群，用于刚体位移的插值。通过指数映射右雅可比的对偶扩展，实现了指定刚体位姿的插值、体对偶扭转及其一阶导数的连续性，并证明了所得曲线具有连续的物理加速度场。文章还区分了代数对偶传递与时间微分延拓，在超对偶代数中分析了高阶全纯性缺陷，并通过对偶对数坐标下的三次或五次Hermite插值消除该缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Park-Ravani构造只能生成SO(3)上的样条，无法直接处理刚体位移（包括旋转和平移）。需要将对偶张量群上的样条插值推广到更一般的刚体运动情形，同时保证物理意义上连续的加速度场，而非仅仅形式化地区分对偶时间量。

Method: 将Park-Ravani构造从SO(3)群形式不变地转移到正交对偶张量群（刚体位移的表示），通过对偶扩展指数映射的右雅可比及其一阶Fréchet导数来紧凑表述递推关系。利用对偶空间扭转的高阶刚体运动学证明物理加速度场的连续性，并在超对偶代数中分析代数对偶传递与时间微分延拓的同时性使用，定义一阶全纯性缺陷，通过立方或五次Hermite插值（在对偶对数坐标中）消除缺陷。

Result: 成功将对偶化Park-Ravani构造推广到刚体位移插值，实现指定位姿插值及体对偶扭转及其一阶导数的连续性，证明物理加速度场连续。非交换三姿态示例验证了递推关系、所有节点连续性陈述以及从米到毫米的维度协变性。发现并分析了超对偶插值的一阶全纯性缺陷，给出了精确反例，并通过Hermite插值方法消除了该缺陷。

Conclusion: Park-Ravani构造可以形式不变地转移到正交对偶张量群上，为刚体位移插值提供了具有连续物理加速度场的样条方法。代数对偶传递与时间微分延拓是两种不同的操作，需要超对偶代数来处理其同时性使用。超对偶插值存在全纯性缺陷，但可通过在对偶对数坐标下进行高阶Hermite插值来消除。

Abstract: The Park-Ravani construction generates a twice continuously differentiable, frame-invariant spline on SO(3) by exponentiating cubic canonical-coordinate polynomials. We show that the construction transfers, without changing form, to the group of orthogonal dual tensors, a representation of rigid displacements. The transferred recurrence is stated compactly through the dual extension of the right Jacobian of the exponential map and its first Fréchet derivative. This yields interpolation of prescribed rigid poses and continuity of the body dual twist and its first derivative. Using the higher-order rigid-body kinematics of dual spatial twists, we then prove that the resulting curve has a continuous physical acceleration field, not merely a continuous quantity obtained by formally differentiating the dual part of a twist. We also distinguish algebraic dual transfer from temporal differential prolongation: their simultaneous first-order use takes place in a hyper-dual algebra, and interpolation of arbitrary prolonged nodal data need not be holonomic. A noncommuting three-pose example verifies the recurrence, all knot continuity statements, and dimensional covariance under a change from meters to millimeters. We define and analyze the first-order holonomy defect of a generic hyper-dual interpolant, exhibit an exact counterexample, and remove the defect by cubic or quintic Hermite interpolation in dual logarithmic coordinates.

</details>


### [271] [Brain-Language-Action (BLA) Models: Language-Conditioned EEG for Robotics Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28967)
*Alexandr Plashchinsky*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Brain-Language-Action (BLA)框架，利用语言条件作用将少量可区分的EEG脑状态动态映射到更大的动作空间。在BCI Competition IV 2a数据集上实现无人机控制概念验证，用4类运动想象EEG生成脑token嵌入，与预训练LLM联合微调，在840种语言定义映射下达到90%的逐token准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统EEG机器人控制将神经信号直接分类映射到固定离散动作集，但EEG信号可分性有限且噪声高，难以扩展到细粒度机器人控制空间。作者希望用语言条件作用解耦"神经状态识别"与"动作映射"，使少量神经类别覆盖更大的动作空间。

Method: 两阶段训练：1) 评估多种EEG编码器架构，将250Hz、3.5秒、22通道的运动想象EEG样本转换为5个128维脑token嵌入；2) 将嵌入投影到预训练LLM的嵌入空间，与语言指令联合微调，自回归生成结构化三token无人机动作。

Result: 在840种可能的语言定义映射（4个神经状态×7种飞行动作组合）中实现90%的逐token准确率，证明语言条件作用可扩展EEG机器人界面的有效控制范围，无需增加可直接区分的神经状态数量。

Conclusion: 这是语言条件作用扩展EEG神经接口控制能力的初步验证，证明少量可可靠区分的脑状态可通过语言定义的映射动态关联不同动作，为更广泛的人机交互接口提供新思路。但仍是概念验证，需要更多实际部署验证。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG)-based robotic control is commonly formulated as a direct classification problem, in which electrical neural signals are mapped to a fixed set of discrete actions.
